6: export const getFileSource = (fileName: string): "csv" | "excel" | "pdf" | "unknown" => {
7:   const lower = fileName.toLowerCase()
8:   if (lower.endsWith(".csv")) return "csv"
9:   if (lower.endsWith(".xlsx") || lower.endsWith(".xls")) return "excel"
10:   if (lower.endsWith(".pdf")) return "pdf"
11:   return "unknown"
12: }
13: 
14: export const extractTransactions = async (
15:   file: File
16: ): Promise<ExtractResult> => {
17:   const source = getFileSource(file.name)
18:   const fileName = file.name
19: 
20:   if (source === "unknown") {
21:     return {
22:       transactions: [],
23:       errors: [
24:         `Incompatible file format: "${fileName}". Use .csv, .xlsx, .xls or .pdf`,
25:       ],
26:       source: "unknown",
27:       fileName,
28:     }
29:   }
30: 
31:   try {
32:     if (source === "csv") {
33:       return await parseCsv(file, fileName)
34:     }
35: 
36:     if (source === "excel") {
37:       return await parseExcel(file, fileName)
38:     }
39: 
40:     if (source === "pdf") {
41:       return await extractPdfTransactions(file, fileName)
42:     }
43:   } catch (error) {
44:     return {
45:       transactions: [],
46:       errors: [
47:         `Unexpected error processing "${fileName}": ${
48:           error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
49:         }`,
50:       ],
51:       source,
52:       fileName,
53:     }
54:   }
55: 
56:   return {
57:     transactions: [],
58:     errors: ["Unsupported file format"],
59:     source: "unknown",
60:     fileName,
61:   }
62: }

64: /**
65:  * Extrae texto del PDF en el cliente usando pdfjs-dist y luego aplica el parser de Atlántida.
66:  */
67: const extractPdfTransactions = async (
68:   file: File,
69:   fileName: string
70: ): Promise<ExtractResult> => {
71:   // Import dinámico para evitar problemas de SSR con pdfjs-dist
72:   const pdfjsLib = await import("pdfjs-dist")
73:   const worker = await import("pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs")
74: 
75:   if (pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc === undefined) {
76:     pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = (worker as any).default || worker
77:   }
78: 
79:   const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
80:   const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer }).promise
81: 
82:   let fullText = ""
83:   for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
84:     const page = await pdf.getPage(i)
85:     const content = await page.getTextContent()
86:     const pageText = content.items
87:       .map((item: any) => (item as any).str ?? "")
88:       .join(" ")
89:     fullText += pageText + "\n"
90:   }
91: 
92:   if (!fullText.trim()) {
93:     return {
94:       transactions: [],
95:       errors: [
96:         "The PDF does not contain readable text. It may be a scanned or protected document.",
97:       ],
98:       source: "pdf",
99:       fileName,
100:       rawText: "",
101:     }
102:   }
103: 
104:   const result = parseAtlántidaPDF(fullText, fileName)
105:   return {
106:     ...result,
107:     rawText: fullText,
108:   }
109: }

20: function xlsxFile(rows: (string | number)[][], name = "movs.xlsx"): File {
21:   const worksheet = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rows)
22:   const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new()
23:   XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, "Movimientos")
24:   const buffer = XLSX.write(workbook, {
25:     type: "array",
26:     bookType: "xlsx",
27:   }) as ArrayBuffer
28: 
29:   return new File([buffer], name, {
30:     type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
31:   })
32: }

20:   SelectContent,
21:   SelectGroup,
22:   SelectItem,
23:   SelectTrigger,
24:   SelectValue,
25: } from "@/components/ui/select"
26: import {
27:   Table,
28:   TableBody,
29:   TableCell,
30:   TableHead,
31:   TableHeader,
32:   TableRow,
33: } from "@/components/ui/table"
34: import type { BankAccounts } from "@/types/bank-accounts.types"
35: import type { Category } from "@/types/categories.types"
36: import {
37:   extractTransactions,
38:   /* getFileSource, */
39: } from "@/lib/parsers/file-extractor"
40: import type { ParsedTransaction } from "@/lib/parsers/types"
41: import { BulkTransactionActionState } from "@/types/transactions.types"
42: import { createBulkTransactions } from "@/server/transactions/actions"
43: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
44: import { CURRENCY_OPTIONS } from "@/lib/utils"
45: import { SuggestionConfidence } from "@/types/ai.types"
46: import { suggestTransactionCategories } from "@/server/transactions/ai-actions"
47: import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch"
48: import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"
49: import { GeminiIcon } from "@/components/icons/gemini"
50: 
51: interface UploadBulkTransactionFormProps {
52:   bankAccounts: BankAccounts[]
53:   categories: Category[]
54:   defaultCurrency: string
55: }
56: 
57: export function UploadBulkTransactionForm({
58:   bankAccounts,
59:   categories,
60:   defaultCurrency,
61: }: UploadBulkTransactionFormProps) {
62:   const [selectedAccountId, setSelectedAccountId] = useState<string>("")
63:   const [transactionCurrency, setTransactionCurrency] = useState<string>(() =>
64:     CURRENCY_OPTIONS.some((option) => option.value === defaultCurrency)
65:       ? defaultCurrency
66:       : "HNL"
67:   )
68:   const [currencyTouched, setCurrencyTouched] = useState(false)
69:   const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
70:   const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<ParsedTransaction[]>([])
71:   const [errors, setErrors] = useState<string[]>([])
72:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)
73:   const [parsedSource, setParsedSource] = useState<
74:     "csv" | "excel" | "pdf" | "unknown" | null
75:   >(null)
76:   const router = useRouter()
77:   const [isSuggesting, setIsSuggesting] = useState(false)
78:   const [overwriteCategories, setOverwriteCategories] = useState(false)
79:   const [suggestionMeta, setSuggestionMeta] = useState<
80:     Record<string, { confidence: SuggestionConfidence; reason: string | null }>
81:   >({})
82:   /*   const [debugInfo, setDebugInfo] = useState<{
83:     fileName: string
84:     source: string
85:     rawText?: string
86:     transactionCount: number
87:   } | null>(null) */
88: 
89:   const handleAccountChange = useCallback(
90:     (accountId: string) => {
91:       setSelectedAccountId(accountId)
92: 
93:       if (currencyTouched) return
94:       const account = bankAccounts.find((acc) => String(acc.id) === accountId)
95:       const accountCurrency = account?.accountCurrency
96:       if (
97:         accountCurrency &&
98:         CURRENCY_OPTIONS.some((option) => option.value === accountCurrency)
99:       ) {
100:         setTransactionCurrency(accountCurrency)
101:       }
102:     },
103:     [bankAccounts, currencyTouched]
104:   )
105: 
106:   const formDispatcher = async (
107:     prevState: BulkTransactionActionState,
108:     formData: FormData
109:   ) => {
110:     formData.append("accountId", selectedAccountId)
111:     formData.append("transactionsJson", JSON.stringify(transactions))
112:     formData.delete("file")
113: 
114:     return createBulkTransactions(prevState, formData)
115:   }
116: 
117:   const [state, formAction, isPending] = useActionState(formDispatcher, null)
118: 
119:   useEffect(() => {
120:     if (!state) return
121: 
122:     if (state.savedTempIds.length > 0) {
123:       const savedTempIds = new Set(state.savedTempIds)
124:       setTransactions((prev) => {
125:         const remaining = prev.filter((tx) => !savedTempIds.has(tx.tempId))
126:         return remaining.length === prev.length ? prev : remaining
127:       })
128:     }
129: 
130:     if (state.success) {
131:       toast.success(state.message)
132:       router.push("/transactions")
133:       return
134:     }
135: 
136:     toast.error(state.message)
137:   }, [state, router])
138: 
139:   const handleFileChange = useCallback(
140:     async (files: File[]) => {
141:       const selectedFile = files[0]
142:       if (!selectedFile) return
143:       if (!selectedAccountId || selectedAccountId === "none") {
144:         toast.error("Select a bank account before uploading the file")
145:         setFile(null)
146:         return
147:       }
148: 
149:       setFile(selectedFile)
150:       setIsLoading(true)
151:       setSuggestionMeta({})
152:       setErrors([])
153: 
154:       try {
155:         const result = await extractTransactions(selectedFile)
156: 
157:         if (result.errors.length > 0 && result.transactions.length === 0) {
158:           setErrors(result.errors)
159:           result.errors.forEach((e) => toast.error(e))
160:           setTransactions([])
161:           setParsedSource(null)
162:           /* setDebugInfo({
163:             fileName: result.fileName,
164:             source: result.source,
165:             rawText: result.rawText,
166:             transactionCount: 0,
167:           }) */
168:           return
169:         }
170: 
171:         setTransactions(result.transactions)
172:         setParsedSource(result.source)
173:         /*  setDebugInfo({
174:           fileName: result.fileName,
175:           source: result.source,
176:           rawText: result.rawText,
177:           transactionCount: result.transactions.length,
178:         }) */
179:         if (result.errors.length > 0) {
180:           result.errors.forEach((e) => toast.warning(e))
181:         }
182:         toast.success(
183:           `Extracted ${result.transactions.length} transaction${
184:             result.transactions.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""
185:           } from file`
186:         )
187:       } catch (error) {
188:         const msg =
189:           error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error reading file"
190:         setErrors([msg])
191:         toast.error(msg)
192:         /*  setDebugInfo({
193:           fileName: selectedFile.name,
194:           source: getFileSource(selectedFile.name),
195:           rawText: undefined,
196:           transactionCount: 0,
197:         }) */
198:       } finally {
199:         setIsLoading(false)
200:       }
201:     },
202:     [selectedAccountId]
203:   )
204: 
205:   const updateTransaction = useCallback(
206:     (tempId: string, updates: Partial<ParsedTransaction>) => {
207:       setTransactions((prev) =>
208:         prev.map((t) => (t.tempId === tempId ? { ...t, ...updates } : t))
209:       )
210:     },
211:     []
212:   )
213: 
214:   const removeTransaction = useCallback((tempId: string) => {
215:     setTransactions((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.tempId !== tempId))
216:   }, [])
217: 
218:   const clearAll = useCallback(() => {
219:     setTransactions([])
220:     setFile(null)
221:     setParsedSource(null)
222:     setErrors([])
223:     setSuggestionMeta({})
224:   }, [])
225: 
226:   const hasTransactions = transactions.length > 0
227: 
228:   const failedRows = (() => {
229:     if (!state?.rows.length) return []
230:     const currentTempIds = new Set(transactions.map((tx) => tx.tempId))
231:     const seen = new Set<string>()
232: 
233:     return state.rows.filter((row) => {
234:       if (
235:         row.success ||
236:         !currentTempIds.has(row.tempId) ||
237:         seen.has(row.tempId)
238:       ) {
239:         return false
240:       }
241: 
242:       seen.add(row.tempId)
243:       return true
244:     })
245:   })()
246: 
247:   const handleSuggestCategories = useCallback(async () => {
248:     const candidates = transactions.filter(
249:       (tx) =>
250:         tx.transactionDescription.trim().length > 0 &&
251:         (overwriteCategories || !tx.categoryId)
252:     )
253:     if (candidates.length === 0) {
254:       toast.info("All rows already have a category")
255:       return
256:     }
257:     setIsSuggesting(true)
258:     try {
259:       const result = await suggestTransactionCategories(
260:         candidates.map((tx) => ({
261:           tempId: tx.tempId,
262:           transactionDescription: tx.transactionDescription,
263:           transactionType: tx.transactionType,
264:           amount: tx.amount,
265:         }))
266:       )
267:       if (!result.success) {
268:         toast.error(result.message)
269:         return
270:       }
271:       const currentById = new Map(transactions.map((tx) => [tx.tempId, tx]))
272:       const applicable = new Map<string, string>()
273:       for (const suggestion of result.suggestions) {
274:         if (suggestion.categoryId === null) continue
275:         const current = currentById.get(suggestion.tempId)
276:         if (!current) continue
277:         if (!overwriteCategories && current.categoryId) continue
278:         applicable.set(suggestion.tempId, suggestion.categoryId)
279:       }
280:       setTransactions((prev) =>
281:         prev.map((tx) => {
282:           const categoryId = applicable.get(tx.tempId)
283:           return categoryId ? { ...tx, categoryId } : tx
284:         })
285:       )
286:       setSuggestionMeta((prev) => {
287:         const next = { ...prev }
288:         for (const suggestion of result.suggestions) {
289:           next[suggestion.tempId] = {
290:             confidence: suggestion.confidence,
291:             reason: suggestion.reason,
292:           }
293:         }
294:         return next
295:       })
296:     } catch (error) {
297:       console.error(error)
298:       toast.error("Could not reach the AI service")
299:     } finally {
300:       setIsSuggesting(false)
301:     }
302:   }, [transactions, overwriteCategories])
303: 
304:   return (
305:     <form
306:       className="flex w-full flex-col gap-6"
307:       autoComplete="off"
308:       action={formAction}
309:     >
310:       <Field>
311:         <FieldLabel>Destination bank account</FieldLabel>
312:         <FieldDescription>
313:           Choose the account where imported transactions will be registered.
314:         </FieldDescription>
315:         <FieldGroup className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 py-2 md:grid-cols-2">
316:           <Field>
317:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="bankAccount">Bank account</FieldLabel>
318:             <Select
319:               value={selectedAccountId}
320:               onValueChange={handleAccountChange}
321:               disabled={isLoading || isPending}
322:             >
323:               <SelectTrigger id="bankAccount">
324:                 <SelectValue placeholder="Select account" />
325:               </SelectTrigger>
326:               <SelectContent>
327:                 {bankAccounts.length === 0 ? (
328:                   <SelectItem disabled value="none">
329:                     No bank accounts available
330:                   </SelectItem>
331:                 ) : (
332:                   bankAccounts.map((acc) => (
333:                     <SelectItem key={acc.id} value={String(acc.id)}>
334:                       {acc.bankName} - {acc.accountName || "Unnamed account"}
335:                       {acc.accountCurrency ? ` (${acc.accountCurrency})` : ""}
336:                     </SelectItem>
337:                   ))
338:                 )}
339:               </SelectContent>
340:             </Select>
341:           </Field>
342:           <Field>
343:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="transactionCurrency">Currency</FieldLabel>
344:             <Select
345:               name="transactionCurrency"
346:               value={transactionCurrency}
347:               onValueChange={(value) => {
348:                 setCurrencyTouched(true)
349:                 setTransactionCurrency(value)
350:               }}
351:               disabled={isLoading || isPending}
352:             >
353:               <SelectTrigger id="transactionCurrency">
354:                 <SelectValue placeholder="Select currency" />
355:               </SelectTrigger>
356:               <SelectContent position="popper">
357:                 <SelectGroup>
358:                   {CURRENCY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
359:                     <SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>
360:                       {option.label}
361:                     </SelectItem>
362:                   ))}
363:                 </SelectGroup>
364:               </SelectContent>
365:             </Select>
366:             <FieldDescription>
367:               Applied to every transaction in this upload.
368:             </FieldDescription>
369:           </Field>
370:           {/* <button type="button" onClick={() => handleGemini()}>
371:             Call Gemini
372:           </button> */}
373:         </FieldGroup>
374:         <FieldError />
375:       </Field>
376: 
377:       {/* Paso 2: Dropzone */}
378:       <Field>
379:         <FieldLabel>Bank statement file</FieldLabel>
380:         <FieldDescription>
381:           Upload a PDF, Excel or CSV generated by Banco Atlántida. Movements
382:           will be read and you can review them before saving.
383:         </FieldDescription>
384:         <Dropzone
385:           name="file"
386:           accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls,.pdf"
387:           maxFiles={1}
388:           label="Drag your file here"
389:           disabled={
390:             isLoading || isPending || !selectedAccountId || isSuggesting
391:           }
392:           onChange={handleFileChange}
393:           onRemove={() => setTransactions([])}
394:           hideOnFile
395:         />
396:         <FieldError />
397:       </Field>
398: 
399:       {isLoading && (
400:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
401:           Processing file…
402:         </div>
403:       )}
404: 
405:       {hasTransactions && (
406:         <section className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4 border-t pt-4">
407:           <div className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
408:             <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
409:               Detected transactions ({transactions.length}) - parser used:{" "}
410:               {parsedSource}
411:             </h2>
412: 
413:             <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
414:               <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
415:                 <Switch
416:                   size="sm"
417:                   checked={overwriteCategories}
418:                   onCheckedChange={setOverwriteCategories}
419:                   disabled={isSuggesting}
420:                 />
421:                 Overwrite existing
422:               </label>
423: 
424:               <Button
425:                 type="button"
426:                 variant="secondary"
427:                 size="sm"
428:                 onClick={handleSuggestCategories}
429:                 disabled={isLoading || isPending || isSuggesting}
430:                 className="w-full cursor-pointer sm:w-auto"
431:               >
432:                 {isSuggesting ? <Spinner /> : <GeminiIcon />}
433:                 {isSuggesting ? "Suggesting..." : "Suggest categories with AI"}
434:               </Button>
435:               <Button
436:                 type="button"
437:                 variant="outline"
438:                 size="sm"
439:                 onClick={clearAll}
440:                 disabled={isLoading || isPending}
441:                 className="w-full cursor-pointer sm:w-auto"
442:               >
443:                 Clear table
444:               </Button>
445:               <Button
446:                 type="submit"
447:                 size="sm"
448:                 disabled={isLoading || isPending || isSuggesting}
449:                 className="w-full cursor-pointer sm:w-auto"
450:               >
451:                 Save transactions
452:               </Button>
453:             </div>
454:           </div>
455: 
456:           {errors.length > 0 && (
457:             <div className="rounded-md border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-[12px] text-destructive">
458:               {errors.map((e, i) => (
459:                 <div key={i} className="mb-1 last:mb-0">
460:                   • {e}
461:                 </div>
462:               ))}
463:             </div>
464:           )}
465: 
466:           {failedRows.length > 0 && (
467:             <div className="rounded-md border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-[12px] text-destructive">
468:               <p className="mb-1 font-medium">Rows that could not be saved:</p>
469:               {failedRows.map((row) => (
470:                 <div key={row.tempId} className="mb-1 last:mb-0">
471:                   • Row {row.row ?? "?"}: {row.error ?? "Unknown error"}
472:                 </div>
473:               ))}
474:             </div>
475:           )}
476: 
477:           <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-md border">
478:             <Table>
479:               <TableHeader>
480:                 <TableRow>
481:                   <TableHead className="w-[12%]">Date</TableHead>
482:                   <TableHead className="w-[26%]">Description</TableHead>
483:                   <TableHead className="w-[14%]">Type</TableHead>
484:                   <TableHead className="w-[14%]">Amount</TableHead>
485:                   <TableHead className="w-[18%]">Bank reference</TableHead>
486:                   <TableHead className="w-[12%]">Category</TableHead>
487:                   <TableHead className="w-[8%] text-right">Actions</TableHead>
488:                 </TableRow>
489:               </TableHeader>
490:               <TableBody>
491:                 {transactions.map((tx) => (
492:                   <TableRow key={tx.tempId}>
493:                     <TableCell>
494:                       <DatePickerCell
495:                         value={tx.transactionDate}
496:                         onChange={(date) =>
497:                           updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
498:                             transactionDate: date,
499:                           })
500:                         }
501:                       />
502:                     </TableCell>
503:                     <TableCell>
504:                       <Input
505:                         value={tx.transactionDescription}
506:                         onChange={(e) =>
507:                           updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
508:                             transactionDescription: e.target.value,
509:                           })
510:                         }
511:                         className="h-8 border-none bg-transparent p-0 text-xs focus:ring-0"
512:                       />
513:                     </TableCell>
514:                     <TableCell>
515:                       <Select
516:                         value={tx.transactionType}
517:                         onValueChange={(v) =>
518:                           updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
519:                             transactionType: v as "income" | "expense",
520:                           })
521:                         }
522:                       >
523:                         <SelectTrigger className="h-8 border-none bg-transparent p-0 text-xs focus:ring-0">
524:                           <SelectValue placeholder="Type" />
525:                         </SelectTrigger>
526:                         <SelectContent>
527:                           <SelectItem value="income">
528:                             Income (Credit)
529:                           </SelectItem>
530:                           <SelectItem value="expense">
531:                             Expense (Debit)
532:                           </SelectItem>
533:                         </SelectContent>
534:                       </Select>
535:                     </TableCell>
536:                     <TableCell>
537:                       <Input
538:                         value={tx.amount}
539:                         onChange={(e) =>
540:                           updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
541:                             amount: e.target.value,
542:                           })
543:                         }
544:                         className="h-8 border-none bg-transparent p-0 text-right font-mono text-xs focus:ring-0"
545:                       />
546:                     </TableCell>
547:                     <TableCell>
548:                       <Input
549:                         value={tx.bankReference ?? ""}
550:                         onChange={(e) =>
551:                           updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
552:                             bankReference: e.target.value || null,
553:                           })
554:                         }
555:                         className="h-8 border-none bg-transparent p-0 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground focus:ring-0"
556:                         placeholder="No reference"
557:                       />
558:                     </TableCell>
559:                     <TableCell>
560:                       <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
561:                         <Select
562:                           value={tx.categoryId ?? "none"}
563:                           onValueChange={(v) => {
564:                             updateTransaction(tx.tempId, {
565:                               categoryId:
566:                                 v === "none" ? undefined : v || undefined,
567:                             })
568:                             setSuggestionMeta((prev) => {
569:                               if (!prev[tx.tempId]) return prev
570:                               const next = { ...prev }
571:                               delete next[tx.tempId]
572:                               return next
573:                             })
574:                           }}
575:                         >
576:                           <SelectTrigger className="h-8 border-none bg-transparent p-0 text-xs focus:ring-0">
577:                             <SelectValue placeholder="Category" />
578:                           </SelectTrigger>
579:                           <SelectContent>
580:                             <SelectItem value="none">No category</SelectItem>
581:                             {categories.map((c) => (
582:                               <SelectItem
583:                                 key={c.categoryId}
584:                                 value={String(c.categoryId)}
585:                               >
586:                                 {c.name}
587:                               </SelectItem>
588:                             ))}
589:                           </SelectContent>
590:                         </Select>
591:                         {tx.categoryId && suggestionMeta[tx.tempId] && (
592:                           <span
593:                             title={
594:                               suggestionMeta[tx.tempId].reason ??
595:                               "Suggested by AI"
596:                             }
597:                             className="shrink-0 rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-muted-foreground"
598:                           >
599:                             IA
600:                           </span>
601:                         )}
602:                       </div>
603:                     </TableCell>
604:                     <TableCell className="text-right">
605:                       <Button
606:                         type="button"
607:                         variant="ghost"
608:                         size="icon"
609:                         onClick={() => removeTransaction(tx.tempId)}
610:                         disabled={isLoading}
611:                         aria-label={`Remove ${tx.transactionDescription}`}
612:                       >
613:                         <span className="sr-only">Remove</span>
614:                         <Trash2Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-destructive" />
615:                       </Button>
616:                     </TableCell>
617:                   </TableRow>
618:                 ))}
619:               </TableBody>
620:             </Table>
621:           </div>
622:         </section>
623:       )}
624: 
625:       {!hasTransactions && !file && !isLoading && (
626:         <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
627:           Select an account and upload a file to get started.
628:         </div>
629:       )}
630:       {/* 
631:       {debugInfo && (
632:         <details className="mt-6 border border-muted/30 rounded-md p-4 text-xs bg-muted/50">
633:           <summary className="font-semibold text-muted-foreground cursor-pointer">
634:             Debug info (raw extracted text)
635:           </summary>
636:           <div className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground break-all whitespace-pre-wrap font-mono text-[10px]">
637:             {debugInfo.rawText ?? "(no text extracted)"}
638:           </div>
639:         </details>
640:       )} */}
641:     </form>
642:   )
643: }

Now I have a good picture. Let me analyze the parsers for flaws and design a generic detection architecture.

Key issues I can identify:

1. **Triplicated helpers**: `cleanAmount`, `normalizeDate`, `MONTH_ES`, `findColumnIndex` duplicated in three files. `parseCsv` and `parseExcel` share near-identical row-mapping logic. That's a bug farm: e.g., cleanAmount differs? No, identical. But date formats differ: excel has serial support, csv doesn't (CSV with Excel serial won't parse).

2. **`cleanAmount` correctness issues**:
   - `"1.234"` (European thousands, no decimals) → stays "1.234" → parsed as 1.23. Wrong (should be 1234). Also `"1,234"` → parts length 2 with 3 digits → removes comma → 1234 correct. Asymmetric.
   - Only strips `L$€`; doesn't strip "HNL", "USD", "Q", "₡", "CRC", "MXN", "ARS" etc. Though `[^\d.,-]` strips all non-digit chars anyway, so currency letters are removed, but the issue is currency labels that contain digits? none. However removing all non-digit except `.,-` means parentheses negatives `(500.00)` lose the sign — a very common accounting format for negatives. Big miss.
   - Doesn't detect minus sign variants (−, –, trailing minus "500.00-").
   - Amount `"L 0.00"` returns "0.00" fine.
   - `cleanAmount("")` → "0" ok.
   - Returns "0" for garbage instead of null, making it impossible to distinguish "no amount" from "zero amount". Type info loss. E.g., a row with "-" becomes 0.00 → expense 0. Current code then includes transactions with amount 0 in debit/credit branch. Should be skipped.
   - `parseFloat(v)` with value like "1.234.567" → NaN → "0". European thousands with multiple dots mis-handled.
   - Indian lakh format untouched.
   - The `[L$€]\s*` replace is redundant given the general strip.

3. **Amount sign → type inference** in CSV/Excel: `amount = cleanAmount(amountRaw).replace(/^-/, "")` — only strips leading `-`; if amount is negative expressed via parentheses, cleanAmount dropped the sign. And `type = parseFloat(amountRaw.replace(/[^\d.-]/g, "")) < 0` — this re-parses raw with a different method than cleanAmount, which can disagree. E.g. European "1.234,56" with negative "-1.234,56" → digits "1.234,56"? no, `[^\d.-]` keeps digits, dot and minus: "-1.234,56" → wait comma removed → "-1.23456" → parseFloat = -1.23456 → negative okay but... it happens to work sign-wise. But "L 500.00-" → 500.00 → income, wrong. Also for `amountRaw` with parentheses → income, wrong. And amount positive/negative semantics: many bank CSVs have "monto" always positive with separate type column ("tipo", "cargo/abono", "D/C"). Not supported.
   - Also: debit/credit branch: if both credit and debit > 0 picks credit. If debit is negative-column style (e.g., "Débito" storing -500), `debit = -500`, not > 0 → falls to amount 0 expense. Row kept with amount "0.00" and no error! Data-quality bug: rows with zero amount are silently accepted.
   - `parseFloat(cleanAmount(...))` — cleanAmount returns a string "1234.56", fine.

4. **Date parsing** gaps:
   - No `DD-MM-YYYY`, `DD.MM.YYYY` (German/European), `MM/DD/YYYY` ambiguity (US), `DD/MM/YY`, `YYYY/MM/DD`, `MM/DD/YY`, ISO with time `2026-09-07T00:00:00Z`, datetime "2026-09-07 00:00:00" from bank exports, "7 Sep 2026", "Sep 7, 2026", "07-SEP-26", "SEP 07", two-digit years, single digit months `7/9/2026`.
   - `MONTH_ES` only Spanish abbreviations; no English (`jan...dec`), no full month names, no `sept`, no Portuguese/French.
   - Ambiguity: `01/02/2026` — assumed DD/MM. In LatAm that's typically right, but for US bank exports wrong → could produce invalid dates or swapped day/month. Needs locale heuristic and validation (reject month > 12 → swap). Currently `normalizeDate("13/45/2026")` returns "2026-45-13" — no validation at all! Invalid date accepted. Big quality issue. Similarly "31/02/2026" accepted.
   - Excel serial range hardcoded 40000–60000 (~2009–2064) — dates before 2009 or after 2064 break. Also `Date.UTC(1899,11,30)` correct for serial, but Excel's 1904 date system (Mac) not handled. Also serial as string in CSV not handled.
   - Time component stripped, fine, but date + time strings fail entirely.

5. **Header detection weaknesses**:
   - `findColumnIndex` uses `includes` on lowercased header; candidate order matters and first match wins. `["fecha","date","fec"]` — "Fecha de liquidación" vs "Fecha" — could pick wrong date column (e.g., "Fecha valor" vs "Fecha operación"). No preference for exact matches, no scoring, no exclusion of "saldo"/"balance".
   - For **amount**: candidates `["monto","amount","valor","total"]` — "Total" may match a summary column; also "Saldo" not matched (good) but "Saldo" contains "sal"? no. However `"total"` is risky. Also fails on "Importe", "Monto en L", "Cargo/Abono", "Importe HNL", "Amount (USD)".
   - Debit/credit candidates lack: "debe", "haber", "cargos", "abonos", "retiros", "depósitos", "pa", "cargo", "abono", "egresos", "ingresos", "withdrawal", "deposit", "paid out", "paid in", "money out/in", "debit amount", "D", "C".
   - No detection of a single signed-amount column vs separate columns vs "type" column.
   - **No header row detection in CSV** — assumes row 1 is header. Bank CSVs often have 3-10 metadata lines first ("Banco X, Estado de cuenta, ..." then blank then headers), or `sep=,` line (Excel), or semicolon-delimited with BOM. Papa with header:true on a metadata preamble → garbage headers → zero columns → all rows "invalid date". No fallback attempts (delimiter autodetect yes Papa does, but header row no).
   - No BOM handling (`\uFEFF` in first header) — `h.trim()` doesn't remove BOM. `"Fecha"` won't match `"fecha"`? Actually `\uFEFFfecha`.includes("fecha") → true, so ok for includes. But exact matches would break.
   - Excel: only first sheet; multi-sheet exports put movements on sheet 2 or per-account; header detection only looks for cells exactly equal to date/transactiondate/fecha, so a header cell "Fecha de operación" fails → "No se encontró la fila de cabecera". Also merged cells; `sheet_to_json` header:1 ignores merged. Also `raw: false` not set → dates come as serials (handled) but formatted currency strings come as numbers with decimals; `String(cell)` on a date cell with type 'd' → "Mon Sep 07 2026..."? Actually with `header:1` and default `raw:true`, dates come as serial numbers, so fine. But if `raw:false`, strings. Currently fine but fragile.
   - Excel `String(row[idx] ?? "")` on numbers gives "500" fine; on booleans; on numbers like 1.5e3? fine.
   - Excel rowNum math is wrong: `rowNum = i + 2` where rows start after header at any position — should be `headerRowIdx + i + 2`. Off-by-N for files with metadata preamble (Atlántida's 5 rows). Affects error messages and displayed row numbers. Same in CSV (nested preamble would break).

6. **PDF parsing is the weakest**:
   - Only Atlántida, hardcoded regexes. Text extracted with `content.items.map(str).join(" ")` — destroys line structure entirely; the regex relies on `\s+` and works only because fields separated by spaces. Fails when description contains "L 500.00"-like patterns, when amounts are adjacent without spaces (pdf.js items usually separate numbers), when page headers interleave, when columns are ordered Debit/Credit/Balance with empty values (e.g., "L 0.00"), when balance negative or with "-", when the PDF has a currency column with "$" instead of "L", when dozens separator is `,` with 2 decimals, when description wraps to a second line, when the table has more than 6 columns (e.g., "Valor en letras", "Sucursal"), or when there's a "N° doc" before date.
   - FORMATO_2_REGEX is dangerously generic: `(\d{4}-[A-Za-z]{3}-\d{1,2})\s+(\S+)\s+(.+?)\s+([\d.,]+)\s+([\d.,]+)\s+([\d.,]+)` — will match any 3 trailing numbers. Descriptions with numbers at end (e.g., "PAGO TARJETA 1234 500.00 0.00 1500.00")? Might over/under capture but the `.+?` lazy plus backtracking tends to grab minimal. Risk: balance column only present in the last, and `\s+` requires spaces. Also FORMATO_1 requires literal "L" prefix; the app's own Atlántida CSV/PDF variants may use "L." or "HNL".
   - No cross-page table continuation, no handling of repeated headers.
   - No use of positional (x,y) information from pdf.js — the proper fix is reconstructing rows by y-coordinate and columns by x-coordinate. That's the big quality win: `getTextContent()` gives `transform` per item. Group items by y (tolerance), sort by x, then infer column boundaries from header positions or gap sizes. Much more robust than regex-on-single-string.
   - Scanned PDFs: no OCR → currently just errors. Could suggest OCR (tesseract) but heavy in browser; maybe out of scope, but can detect "no text layer" and give clear message.
   - Encrypted PDFs: `getDocument` needs `password` param → throws; message generic.

7. **Detection/architecture gaps for "generic formats and other banks"**:
   - `getFileSource` only by extension; no MIME sniffing; file named `estado.txt` (tab-delimited) unsupported; `.xls` HTML-disguised files (some banks export HTML as .xls) → XLSX.read may handle? sometimes fails.
   - No `ExtractResult` metadata about what was detected: bank, currency, column mapping, confidence, warnings. UI shows "parser used: pdf" only.
   - No confidence scoring; no fallback chain (try Atlántida → generic table → error).
   - No currency detection (`L`, `HNL`, `Q`, `$`, `USD`, `EUR`, `COP`), though the form has a currency selector defaulting to account currency. Could auto-detect and prefill.
   - No dedup/idempotency: tempId includes `Date.now()`/Math.random → re-uploading same statement duplicates transactions; and `bankReference` could be used as a dedupe key. Not parser-per-se but relevant quality.
   - No balance reconciliation check: parsers ignore the balance column. A powerful validation: running balance should equal previous + credit − debit. If mismatch, warn. Also "saldo anterior" line gives starting balance. This detects column misdetection (debit/credit swapped) — high quality signal.
   - No credit-card statement handling (purchase vs payment, "Cuotas", "Intereses", MSI) — different concept. Maybe out of scope.
   - No handling of bank-specific "type" text columns ("TRANSFERENCIA", "PAGO", "RETIRO ATM") to infer type when amount is always positive.
   - No handling of foreign-currency transactions (original amount + USD amount columns).
   - No `status`/`pending` filtering ("Pendiente", "Autorizado").
   - Columns like "Ref." with spaces, "N° Documento", "Documento/Referencia".
   - Multi-line descriptions in CSV (quoted newlines) — Papa handles quotes fine, but PDFs don't.
   - Encoding: CSV in Latin-1/CP1252 (very common from LatAm bank exports) → `file.text()` decodes as UTF-8 → "DescripciÃ³n" → header matching fails. Should attempt UTF-8 then fall back to `TextDecoder("windows-1252")`. Also UTF-16LE with BOM (Excel "Unicode Text" .txt). This is a real, common failure. Papa also has `encoding` option but with ArrayBuffer.
   - Excel `.xls` vs `.xlsx` handled by XLSX.
   - `XLSX` in client bundle is heavy (~400KB) — could lazy-import. Not quality but perf.

8. **Tests**: only 31 tests but no tests for tricky money/date formats; also tests pass `parseCsv` with default 1-row header only. The plan should include test fixtures for each bank/messy input, including golden files.

Let me verify a couple of specifics to avoid false claims:

1. `cleanAmount("1.234")` → v has "." no "," → skips both branches → parseFloat("1.234") = 1.234 → "1.23". So 1234 becomes 1.23. Confirmed bug.
2. `cleanAmount("(1,234.56)")` → strips non [\d.,-] → "1,234.56" → both present, lastComma<lastDot → remove commas → 1234.56 positive. Sign lost. Confirmed.
3. `normalizeDate("31/02/2026")` → matches regex → "2026-02-31" invalid date string, passes to schema? The bulk schema refines `new Date("2026-02-31T00:00:00")` → JS normalizes to Mar 3, valid! So invalid dates silently become wrong dates. Confirmed serious.
4. `normalizeDate("7/9/2026")` → `\d{1,2}` matches single digits → ok actually. Good.
5. CSV without amount but with sign column — not supported. Confirmed.
6. Excel rowNum off-by: `rowNum = i + 2` where i is index in rows slice; for header at index 5, actual sheet row = 5 + i + 2 = i + 7. So reported row is wrong by 5. Confirmed.
7. CSV encoding: `file.text()` always UTF-8 per spec. Latin-1 → mojibake. Then header "DescripciÃ³n" won't match "descripción"... note: "descripción" candidate is the third option; "descripcion" won't match mojibake either. So description column not found → descriptions become "No description". Confirmed major.
8. Excel `String(cell)` where cell is a Date object? With `type: "array"` and `sheet_to_json(header:1)` default `raw:true`, cellDates false → dates as numbers. OK.
9. `xlsx` 0.18.5 — `sheet_to_json` supports `raw`, `defval`, `blankrows`, `range`. Confirmed from memory; fine.
10. pdfjs v5: `getTextContent()` items: TextItem {str, dir, transform, width, height, fontName, hasEOL}. Confirmed by d.ts above. So positional reconstruction feasible. Also `GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc` setup. Also note in v5 worker is `.mjs` and they import it; fine.

One more: FORMATO_1 regex requires two L amounts + balance; if the statement has empty debit shown as "L 0.00" fine. If description contains "L" followed by digits? unlikely.

Potential subtle issue: FORMATO_2 regex could match the *last three* numbers of a row that actually has 4 numeric columns (e.g., reference numeric like "00001234"? ref is `\S+`). Consider "2026-Sep-07 12345 PAGO 500.00 0.00 1,500.00" → works. But if row is "2026-Sep-07 12345 PAGO TARJETA 1234 500.00 0.00 1,500.00", then desc captures "PAGO TARJETA 1234"? Lazy `.+?` with trailing 3 number groups: it would try desc minimal but must leave 3 numbers → desc = "PAGO TARJETA", then groups 1234, 500.00, 0.00 → debit=1234 wrong, credit=500 → income wrongly! Actually order: match `\s+([\d.,]+)\s+([\d.,]+)\s+([\d.,]+)` greedy on last? `[\d.,]+` is greedy but must have 3 separated groups and then end-of-match isn't anchored, so it can match at first possibility: desc="PAGO TARJETA", debit=1234, credit=500.00, balance=0.00. So a description with a trailing number corrupts debit/credit. Confirmed real risk. Since not anchored to line end and no line structure.

Also dedupe key: `${dateRaw}-${ref}-${debitRaw}-${creditRaw}` — two identical transactions same day same amount same ref null? Fine.

Another: parseAtlántidaPDF uses `transactions.length + 1` for row → row numbers don't correspond to source; combined with f1/f2 both numbering. Minor.

Also `tempId` uses `Math.random` and `Date.now` → unstable across renders? It's created once at parse; fine. But for re-upload dedupe, not usable.

So the "Could not read CSV" branch is dead in practice; parsing errors appear in `result.errors` and are ignored entirely! Papa returns `result.errors` (e.g., TooFewFields/Quotes) — parser doesn't surface them. Confirmed: `complete: (result) => { ... resolve(...) }` ignores `result.errors`. Quality issue: silently truncated rows.

Also `header:true` with duplicate headers → Papa renames with `_1` suffix; fine.

Also CSV delimiter: Papa auto-detects among `,` `;` `\t` `|`? Papa's auto-detect uses delimitersToGuess default `[',', '\t', '|', ';']`. Actually default is `[",", "\t", "|", ";", RECORD_SEP, UNIT_SEP]`. So semicolon OK. But `sep=;` line from Excel breaks things (header becomes sep=;). Should strip.

Also for CSV, decimal comma with semicolon delimiter: "1.234,56" handled by cleanAmount. OK.

Excel: no support for multiple sheets/fallback; no `defval`.

Another generic gap: many LatAm banks export "Estado de cuenta" as PDF with columns Fecha | Descripción | Monto | Saldo where Monto has sign. Not supported (needs 3 numbers). And credit card PDFs with "Fecha de compra | Fecha de posteo | Referencia | Descripción | Monto" (4-5 cols, no debit/credit) — not supported.

Also no handling of "Monto en dólares" second amount column → naive `findColumnIndex(headers, ["monto"...])` picks first "Monto" which could be USD.

Interesting: `findColumnIndex` header candidates for date include "fec" — "Fecha de posteo" vs "Fecha de compra": first wins by column order, not semantic — for credit cards you want posteo (or compra) consistently.

Now, plan. Propose architecture:

```
lib/parsers/
  core/
    money.ts        parseAmount(raw) -> { value: number|null, negative: boolean, currency?: string }
    dates.ts        parseDate(raw, {dayFirst}) -> { iso, confidence } + validate
    text.ts         decodeFile(file) -> string (UTF-8/UTF-16/CP1252), strip BOM, normalize unicode (NFKC), collapse spaces
    table.ts        detectHeaderRow(rows), mapColumns(headers), normalizeRows(rows, mapping) -> ParsedTransaction[]
    detect.ts       sniff format & bank (signature scoring)
    types.ts        internal DetectionResult { bank, currency, confidence, mapping, warnings }
  adapters/
    atlantida.ts    bank adapter: signatures + column hints (PDF/CSV/XLSX)
    generic-table.ts fallback for CSV/XLSX
    generic-pdf.ts  positional PDF table extraction + generic regex fallback
  pdf/
    layout.ts       items -> rows (y clustering) + columns (x clustering) -> cells
  file-extractor.ts orchestrator: sniff → try adapters by score → fallback chain → aggregates warnings
  types.ts (extended)
```

Extend `ExtractResult` with optional `detection`: `{ format, bank, currency, confidence, headerRow?, delimiter?, encoding?, mapping, warnings }` and maybe `balanceCheck`. Keep backward compatible (tests).

Also per-row: `rawRow`? `warnings?: string[]`. Keep `errors` as strings (tests rely).

Detection algorithm for CSV/Excel:
- Decode & normalize.
- For each candidate header row (first ~30 rows), compute a score: does row have a date-like header, an amount/debit/credit header, a description header? Score columns. Pick best.
- Map columns with exact-then-fuzzy, priority-ordered candidate lists, exclude "saldo/balance" from amount unless nothing else. Distinguish debit/credit by header semantics, and signed single column by sampling data (majority of values negative → signed) or presence of "tipo"/"D/C" column.
- Validate a sample of rows: date parse rate ≥ threshold, amount parse rate; if low, try alternative mapping (swap date columns, try next amount column). Score = parse success ratio − warnings. Pick best mapping. This "self-validating mapping" is the key generic improvement.
- Balance reconciliation if a balance column exists: prev + credit − debit == balance (tolerance 0.01). If fails, try swapped debit/credit. Very strong signal for correctness.

PDF:
- layout.ts: group items by y within tolerance (e.g., 2-3 pt, scaled to font height), sort by x, join cells by gaps > threshold. Output rows of cells.
- Detect header row among rows using the same header mapper (so bank-agnostic for PDFs with a text layer).
- Then map columns using x-ranges from header cells: assign each cell to nearest header column by x-center. This handles multi-word descriptions and numbers in descriptions much better than regex.
- Fallback to regex adapters (Atlántida) if no header detected, then generic date+trailing-numbers regex with the *last* three numeric cells on a line (right-aligned columns), anchored per reconstructed line.
- Multi-page: concatenate row streams; drop repeated headers/footers/page markers via signature.

Money parsing v2:
- Handle `(123.45)`, `123.45-`, unicode minus, "CR"/"DB" suffixes? (some banks), leading currency tokens, "L.", "HNL", "Q", "₡", "CRC", "US$", "USD".
- Decide decimal separator by heuristic based on last separator + digit counts, and if ambiguous (`1.234`) use a hint (locale/column majority) or positive-integer assumption for 3-digit groups.
- Return null for unparseable (vs "0") but keep public `cleanAmount` returning string for compat.
- Strip trailing "D"/"C" only if exactly one char and adjacent to number? risky; skip.

Dates v2:
- Formats: ISO (with T/time), `YYYY/MM/DD`, `DD/MM/YYYY`, `D/M/YY`, `DD-MM-YYYY`, `DD.MM.YYYY`, `DD MMM YYYY`, `MMM DD, YYYY`, `DD-MMM-YY`, `YYYY-MMM-DD`, `MMM YYYY`? (no day → skip), plus Excel/Sheets serials in a wide range (1..80000) and epoch ms.
- Month names: ES + EN (+ abbreviations, "sept", "set"? PT). Accent-insensitive, case-insensitive.
- Validate real calendar date (leap years), reject impossible, and resolve D/M ambiguity: if first > 12 → day-first; if second > 12 → month-first; else use file-level majority vote (per file, compute both interpretations across rows and pick the consistent one), default day-first (LatAm).
- Two-digit year: 00-69 → 2000s, 70-99 → 1900s (configurable).
- Strip time and timezone; if date is future beyond tomorrow, warn (schema forbids future).

CSV v2:
- Read `arrayBuffer`, detect encoding via BOM + heuristic (UTF-8 validity test → else CP1252), decode.
- Strip `sep=<char>` line, BOM, zero-width chars.
- Papa with `delimiter` (or `delimitersToGuess`), `skipEmptyLines: "greedy"`, `newline`, `quoteChar`, and surface `result.errors` as warnings.
- Header row detection (scan up to N rows using raw parse without header, then re-parse with chosen header index via `beforeFirstChunk`? Simpler: parse once with header:false then treat header row manually — that's the cleanest and shares code with Excel generic table). This unifies CSV & Excel into one `parseTable(rows)` path. Big simplification: CSV → matrix, Excel → matrix, both into generic table parser + adapters.

## Diagnóstico: qué está débil hoy

### 1. Código triplicado (riesgo estructural)
`cleanAmount`, `normalizeDate`, `MONTH_ES` y `findColumnIndex` están copiados en `csv-parser.ts`, `excel-parser.ts` y `atlantida-pdf-parser.ts`. Ya divergieron: `excel` soporta seriales de Excel, `csv` no. Y todo el mapeo de filas de CSV/Excel es el mismo código duplicado (~60 líneas), lo que duplica cada bug.

### 2. Bugs de dinero (`cleanAmount`)
| Entrada | Devuelve | Esperado | Problema |
|---|---|---|---|
| `1.234` (miles EU) | `1.23` | `1234.00` | asume decimal |
| `(1,234.56)` | `1234.56` positivo | `-1234.56` | pierde el signo contable |
| `500.00-` | `500.00` positivo | `-500.00` | no soporta menos final |
| `−500.00` (U+2212) | `0.00`/positivo | `-500` | no normaliza unicode |
| `1.234.567` | `0.00` | `1234567` | NaN silencioso |
| `HNL 1,500.00` / `Q 300` / `₡` | funciona por descarte | — | pero se pierde la moneda |

Además devuelve `"0"` ante basura, así que **no se distingue "sin monto" de "monto cero"**. Consecuencia real: en la rama débito/crédito, si `debit` viene negativo (columna "Débito" con `-500`) no entra en ninguna condición y se genera una transacción con `amount: "0.00"` sin error — y el schema de guardado acepta `0.00`. Se guardan filas basura.

El tipo se decide con **otra** expresión distinta (`amountRaw.replace(/[^\d.-]/g,"") < 0`) que no coincide con `cleanAmount`: cualquier error de signo se propaga como ingreso.

### 3. Bugs de fechas (`normalizeDate`)
- **No valida el calendario**: `31/02/2026` → `"2026-02-31"`, y el schema lo acepta porque `new Date("2026-02-31T00:00:00")` normaliza a 3 de marzo. Se guarda una fecha equivocada en silencio.
- Solo 3 formatos. Faltan: `DD-MM-YYYY`, `DD.MM.YYYY`, `YYYY/MM/DD`, `DD/MM/YY`, ISO con hora (`2026-09-07T00:00:00`, muy común en exports), `7 Sep 2026`, `Sep 7, 2026`, `07-SEP-26`, meses en inglés (`jan`…`dec`, `sept`).
- Ambigüedad `01/02/2026` resuelta siempre como día/mes, sin heurística por archivo.
- Serial de Excel limitado a `40000..60000` (2009–2064) y solo en Excel, no en CSV.

### 4. Detección de cabeceras y formato (lo que bloquea "otros bancos")
- **CSV asume que la línea 1 es cabecera**. Los estados de cuenta reales traen preámbulo (nombre del banco, cuenta, período, líneas vacías) o `sep=;`. Resultado: cero columnas → todas las filas "invalid date".
- **Encoding**: `file.text()` siempre decodifica UTF-8. Un CSV en CP1252/Latin-1 (habitual en Latinoamérica) llega como `DescripciÃ³n` → no matchea → todas las descripciones se vuelven "No description". Tampoco soporta UTF-16LE de Excel.
- **Matching de columnas por `includes` con orden de candidatos**, no por puntuación. `["monto","amount","valor","total"]` toma "Monto original" o "Total" antes que la columna correcta; no distingue moneda extranjera.
- Faltan sinónimos: `debe/haber`, `cargos/abonos`, `retiros/depósitos`, `egresos/ingresos`, `importe`, `withdrawal/deposit`, `paid out/in`, `D/C`.
- **No se soporta** una columna única de monto con signo + columna "tipo", ni monto siempre positivo (tarjetas), ni columnas `Saldo` (que hoy se ignoran).
- Excel: solo la primera hoja; cabecera debe ser exactamente `fecha`/`date`/`transactiondate` (falla con "Fecha de operación"); `rowNum = i + 2` está mal cuando hay preámbulo (error de +5 filas en archivos de Atlántida).
- Papa devuelve `result.errors` (comillas sin cerrar, campos de más) y **se descartan**; el callback `error` que usa el test es prácticamente inalcanzable en modo string.
- `if (!description && !bankReference)` es código muerto: `description` nunca es vacío por el `|| "No description"`.

### 5. PDF (la mayor oportunidad de calidad)
- Se hace `items.map(i => i.str).join(" ")`: **se destruye la estructura de líneas y columnas**, y luego se intenta recuperarla con regex sobre el texto plano.
- `FORMATO_2_REGEX` no está anclada a fin de línea y toma "los 3 últimos números" por backtracking. Una descripción con número final rompe la asignación: `... PAGO TARJETA 1234 500.00 0.00 1,500.00` puede leer débito=1234. Riesgo de invertir débito/crédito.
- `FORMATO_1` exige literalmente `L `; falla con `L.`, `HNL`, `US$`, o si pdf.js no inserta espacio entre campos.
- Sin balance reconciliation, sin continuidad entre páginas, sin filtrado robusto de pies de página, sin PDFs cifrados/escaneados (mensaje genérico), sin tarjetas de crédito.

### 6. Ausencias transversales
- No hay metadatos de detección: el UI solo muestra "parser used: pdf". No hay confianza, ni columnas detectadas, ni warnings.
- No hay fallback: si no es Atlántida, falla. No hay sniffing por contenido ni por MIME (un `.txt` tabulado o un `.xls` que en realidad es HTML no se detectan).
- No hay deduplicación al re-subir el mismo estado (tempId con `Date.now()`/`random`), aunque `bankReference` + fecha + monto serían una clave natural.
- Descripciones largas de PDF pueden exceder el `max(255)` de `bulkTransactionSchema` y fallar fila por fila sin aviso previo.
- `xlsx` se importa estático (bundle pesado en cliente).

## Propuesta de arquitectura

La idea central: **normalizar toda fuente a una matriz `string[][]` y un motor único de mapeo/validación**, en vez de tres parsers con lógica propia. Eso es lo que habilita "genérico + otros bancos" sin escribir un parser por banco.

```
lib/parsers/
  core/
    money.ts      parseAmount(raw) -> { value: number|null, sign, currency? }
    dates.ts      parseDate(raw, { dayFirst }) -> { iso, confidence } (valida calendario)
    text.ts       decodeFile() BOM/UTF-8/CP1252/UTF-16, NFKC, limpieza
    table.ts      detectHeaderRow, mapColumns (scoring), rowsToTransactions, reconcileBalance
    detect.ts     puntuación de formatos/bancos + confianza
  pdf/
    layout.ts     TextItem[] -> filas por Y y celdas por X (usa transform/width/hasEOL)
  adapters/
    atlantida.ts  firmas + hints (orden de fecha, moneda, líneas resumen)
    generic-table.ts  CSV/XLSX sin cabecera conocida (inferencia por patrón de datos)
    generic-pdf.ts    header posicional + fallback regex anclado por línea
  file-extractor.ts  orquestador: sniff → adapters por score → fallback chain
```

Claves del diseño:
1. **CSV y XLSX comparten el mismo motor** (Papa en `header:false` → matriz; XLSX → matriz). Se elimina la duplicación y ambos ganan detección de preámbulo, encoding y numeración de fila correcta.
2. **Mapeo auto-validado**: se prueban varias hipótesis de mapeo (columna de fecha A vs B, monto firmado vs débito/crédito vs columna tipo) y se elige la que maximiza la tasa de parseo + cuadre de saldo. Si hay columna `Saldo`, se valida `anterior + crédito − débito ≈ saldo`; si falla, se prueban débito/crédito invertidos y se marca warning. Esto detecta errores de columna sin saber el banco.
3. **PDF posicional**: agrupar items por `transform[5]` (Y) con tolerancia, ordenar por `transform[4]` (X), cortar celdas por huecos > umbral; detectar la fila de cabecera con el mismo `mapColumns` y asignar celdas por rango X. Los regex quedan solo como fallback, anclados por línea reconstruida y tomando los **últimos** grupos numéricos de la línea (columnas alineadas a la derecha).
4. **`ExtractResult` extendido** (opcional y retrocompatible): `detection: { format, bank?, currency?, confidence, headerRow, encoding, mapping, warnings[] }`. El UI puede mostrar "Detectado: Atlántida · CSV · débito/crédito · confianza alta" y un panel de warnings (ya existe el bloque `debugInfo` comentado que sirve de base).
5. **Adaptadores por banco = solo hints**, no lógica nueva: firma de texto ("Banco Atlántida", "Ficohsa", "BAC Credomatic", "Banpaís"…), orden de fecha esperado, moneda, modo tarjeta, líneas a ignorar.

## Fases propuestas

**Fase 0 – Red de seguridad (sin cambio de comportamiento)**
Extraer `core/money.ts`, `core/dates.ts`, `core/table.ts`; re-exportar `cleanAmount`/`normalizeDate` desde `csv-parser.ts` para no romper los 31 tests existentes (L-13…L-31). Añadir fixtures sintéticos en `tests/fixtures/parsers/`.

**Fase 1 – Corrección de bugs (alto impacto, bajo riesgo)**
Signos contables y unicode; `parseAmount` con `null` real; validación de calendario; reemplazo de filas de monto 0 por error; tipo derivado del mismo parser de monto; `rowNum` absoluto en Excel; truncado de descripción a 255 con warning; propagar `result.errors` de Papa como warnings.

**Fase 2 – Motor genérico CSV/XLSX**
Decodificación con fallback CP1252/UTF-16, BOM, `sep=`, preámbulo, detección de cabecera por scoring, sinónimos ampliados, columna tipo / monto firmado / débito-crédito, modo sin cabecera, cuadre de saldo, metadatos de detección.

**Fase 3 – PDF posicional**
`pdf/layout.ts` + `generic-pdf.ts`, filtrado de cabeceras/pies repetidos, multi-página, PDFs sin capa de texto con mensaje claro (OCR fuera de alcance), fallback a los regex actuales ya anclados.

**Fase 4 – Integración UI y extras (opcionales)**
Panel de detección/warnings, prefill de moneda detectada, dedupe intra-archivo y aviso de re-subida por `bankReference`, texto del dropzone sin "Banco Atlántida", `xlsx` en import dinámico.

**Pruebas**: tabla de ~40 casos de dinero/fecha (incluidos los de la tabla de arriba), tests por banco con CSV/XLSX sintéticos (preámbulo, `;` + coma decimal, CP1252, `sep=`, BOM, UTF-16), tests de `layout.ts` con arrays de `TextItem` a mano (sin PDF real), y mantener los L-13…L-31 verdes. Los 5 archivos actuales quedan en ~2.000 líneas totales; no requiere dependencias nuevas (podría usarse `date-fns@4.4` que ya está instalado para validación, aunque prefiero validación propia sin dependencia).

|---|---|
| `lib/parsers/core/money.ts` | `parseAmount(raw) → { value: number \| null, negative, currency }`. Un solo parser de dinero. |
| `lib/parsers/core/dates.ts` | `parseDate(raw, { dayFirst }) → { iso, confidence }` + `resolveDayFirst(samples)`. |
| `lib/parsers/core/text.ts` | `decodeFileBytes()` (BOM → UTF-8 → CP1252 → UTF-16), `normalizeText()` (NFKC, NBSP, guiones unicode, `sep=`). |
| `lib/parsers/core/table.ts` | `Matrix = string[][]`, `detectHeader()`, `mapColumns()`, `extractRows()`, `reconcileBalances()`, `inferHeaderlessMapping()`. |
| `lib/parsers/core/detection.ts` | Tipos `DetectionInfo`, `ParseWarning`; scoring de formatos. |

`lib/parsers/types.ts` se extiende de forma **aditiva**: `ExtractResult.detection?: DetectionInfo`, `ExtractResult.warnings?: string[]`, `ParsedTransaction.rawRow?: string[]`. Nada existente cambia de forma, así que los 31 tests siguen compilando.

Los tres parsers actuales pasan a delegar: `cleanAmount` y `normalizeDate` se mantienen exportados (re-export) con la misma firma para no romper `parsers.spec.ts`.

## Fase 1 · Corrección de bugs (impacto alto, riesgo bajo)

- **Dinero**: paréntesis contables, `500.00-`, `−` U+2212, `1.234` como miles, múltiples separadores, monedas (`L/HNL`, `Q/GTQ`, `₡/CRC`, `$`, `US$`, `€`, `C$`, `RD$`, `S/`). `null` real para "no es monto", en vez de `"0"`.
- **Fechas**: validación de calendario real (rechaza 31/02, 45/13), formatos `DD-MM-YYYY`, `DD.MM.YYYY`, `YYYY/MM/DD`, `DD/MM/YY`, ISO con hora, `7 Sep 2026`, `Sep 7, 2026`, `07-SEP-26`, meses ES+EN, serial/large epoch. Ambigüedad `01/02/2026` resuelta por voto mayoritario del archivo (default día/mes).
- **Tabla**: fila con monto 0 o negativo en débito/crédito → error explícito, no transacción de `0.00`; tipo derivado del **mismo** parseo (se elimina la doble expresión); `rowNum` absoluto (arregla el desfase de +5 en XLSX con preámbulo); descripción truncada a 255 con warning; errores de Papa (`TooFewFields`, comillas) expuestos como warnings; eliminar el `if (!description …)` muerto.
- **Excel**: recorrer todas las hojas, elegir la de mayor score, `defval: ""`.

## Fase 2 · Motor genérico CSV/XLSX (multi-banco real)

1. `file-extractor.ts` detecta por **contenido**, no solo extensión: `PK\x03\x04` → xlsx, `%PDF` → pdf, texto → csv/tabulado; MIME como respaldo.
2. CSV: `arrayBuffer` → `decodeFileBytes` → quitar `sep=;`/BOM → Papa en `header:false` → `Matrix`. Se acaba el "línea 1 es cabecera".
3. Excel: XLSX → `Matrix` (misma ruta).
4. `generic-table.ts` sobre la matriz:
   - escaneo de las primeras ~30 filas y **scoring** de cabecera (fecha + monto/débito-crédito + descripción, penalizando "saldo"/"total");
   - sinónimos ampliados (`debe/haber`, `cargos/abonos`, `retiros/depósitos`, `egresos/ingresos`, `importe`, `withdrawal/deposit`, `paid in/out`);
   - tres modos: columna única firmada, débito/crédito, monto positivo + columna "tipo"/`D`/`C`;
   - **mapeo auto-validado**: se prueban hipótesis alternativas (fecha A vs B, débito/crédito vs monto, swap) y se elige la de mayor tasa de parseo + cuadre de saldo;
   - **cuadre de saldo** `anterior + crédito − débito ≈ saldo`; si falla se prueban columnas invertidas y se emite warning;
   - modo sin cabecera: infiere columnas por patrón de datos.
5. Adaptador `atlantida.ts` solo con *hints* (firma de texto, orden de fecha, moneda, líneas resumen). El resto es el motor genérico.

## Fase 3 · PDF posicional (la mejora más grande)

- `pdf/layout.ts`: `getTextContent()` → agrupar por `transform[5]` (Y) con tolerancia según `height`, ordenar por `transform[4]` (X), cortar celdas por huecos mayores al umbral, usar `hasEOL` como señal auxiliar. Multi-página con detección de cabeceras/pies repetidos ("Página X de Y") y líneas de resumen.
- `generic-pdf.ts`: filas reconstruidas → `mapColumns` sobre la fila de cabecera → asignación de cada celda por **rango X** (no por orden de tokens). Esto arregla descripciones con números (`PAGO TARJETA 1234`) y columnas desalineadas.
- `atlantida-pdf-parser.ts` conserva su firma `(text, fileName)` y sus dos regex como **fallback anclado a línea**, con estrategia de "últimos N grupos numéricos" y validación contra el saldo.
- PDF sin capa de texto / cifrado: mensaje específico y accionable (OCR queda fuera de alcance).

## Fase 4 · Integración UI

- `UploadBulkTransactionForm.tsx`: chip de detección ("Atlántida · PDF · débito/crédito · alta"), panel de warnings, texto del dropzone sin "Banco Atlántida", prefill de moneda detectada (respetando `currencyTouched`).
- `xlsx` en import dinámico dentro de `excel-parser.ts`.
- Aviso de posible duplicado por `bankReference` + fecha + monto (solo cliente, sin tocar DB).

## Tests y criterios de aceptación

- Nuevos: `tests/unit/parsers/money.spec.ts` (≥15 casos), `dates.spec.ts` (≥20), `table.spec.ts` (≥12), `pdf-layout.spec.ts` (≥8, con arrays de `TextItem` a mano, sin PDF real), `fixtures/parsers/` con CSV/XLSX sintéticos (preámbulo, `;`+coma decimal, CP1252, `sep=`, BOM, UTF-16).
- **No se modifica ningún test existente**; L-13…L-31 deben seguir verdes.
- `npm run typecheck` y `npm run lint` limpios.
- Verificación manual: un PDF de Atlántida, un CSV de otro banco y un XLSX con preámbulo → fechas/montos/tipos correctos, numeración de fila correcta y cuadre de saldo informado.

## Riesgos y decisiones ya cerradas

- Sin dependencias nuevas (validación de fechas propia, no hace falta `date-fns`). Sin IA. Detección 100% genérica, sin parsers por banco.
- Los regex del PDF cambian de comportamiento en casos límite; por eso quedan cubiertos por tests antes de reemplazarlos.
- Fixtures reales: si me pasás estados de cuenta, los anonimizo; si no, genero sintéticos que reproduzcan cada caso.

Con esto el directorio `lib/parsers` pasa de ~3 archivos duplicados a un core + adaptadores (~2.000 líneas con tests), y el sistema deja de depender de Atlántida para detectar columnas.

Estoy en **Plan mode**, así que no puedo aplicar cambios todavía. Cuando quieras arrancar, cambiá al agente de implementación y empezamos por la Fase 0, confirmándote antes de cada commit. ¿Querés que deje este plan escrito en `C:\Users\diego\.opencode\plan` para tenerlo como referencia, o lo mantenemos solo en la conversación?

money.ts:

```ts
export type CurrencyCode = "HNL" | "USD" | "EUR" | "GTQ" | "CRC" | "NIO" | "DOP" | "PEN" | "MXN" | "COP" | "ARS" | "CLP" | "PYG" | "BOB" | "UYU" | null

export interface ParsedAmount {
  value: number | null      // signed, 2 decimals rounded, null if unparseable
  negative: boolean
  currency: CurrencyCode
  raw: string
}

export interface ParseAmountOptions {
  /** Hint for ambiguous "1.234" → thousands when true */
  decimalSeparator?: "." | ","
  /** Default currency when the token is missing */
  currency?: CurrencyCode
  /** Treat parentheses as negative (default true) */
  accountingNegatives?: boolean
}
```

Algorithm:
1. If !raw.trim() → null.
2. Normalize: NFKC, replace unicode minus/en dash/em dash with "-", NBSP with space.
3. Detect currency token: search for patterns: `L\b|LPS|HNL|LEMPIRA`, `Q|GTQ|QUETZAL`, `₡|CRC|COLON`, `US\$|USD|\$`, `€|EUR`, `C\$|NIO|CORDOBA`, `RD\$|DOP`, `S\/|PEN`, `MXN|MX\$`, `COP`, `ARS`, `CLP`, `PYG|₲`, `BOB|Bs`, `UYU|\$U`. Careful: "S/" regex. Determine first match by earliest index.
4. Determine negative: leading/trailing "-", parentheses, "DB"/"D" suffix? Skip suffix letters (ambiguous). Also "CR" is credit. Keep it to parentheses + minus.
5. Strip everything except digits, ".", ",", "-". Then remove remaining "-" (we already captured sign).
6. Determine separator:
   - both present → last occurrence wins (decimal), other is thousands → remove all of the other, replace decimal with ".".
   - only one type:
     - count occurrences. If >1 → all are thousands (e.g., 1.234.567) → remove all.
     - single occurrence: look at digits after it.
       - 3 digits after and the part before is 1-3 digits → ambiguous: thousands (e.g., "1.234", "12.345") — but "1.500" could be 1.5 with 3 decimals (rare in banking). Default to thousands; if `decimalSeparator` hint matches, treat as decimal.
       - 1-2 digits after → decimal.
       - 4+ digits after → thousands? e.g., "1.2345" → weird → treat as decimal (parseFloat handles).
       - 0 digits after → thousands (trailing separator) → remove.
     - Actually for "0.00" → 2 digits → decimal. Good.
   - neither → integer.
7. Parse float; guard NaN, Infinity; round to 2 decimals using Math.round(v*100)/100 (avoid float artifacts) — better: keep as number and let toFixed handle. Return value with sign applied.

Edge: "L 3,300.00" → strip letters: "3300.00"? The comma is thousands and dot decimal → 3300.00. Good.
Edge: "1,234" → 3 digits after comma → thousands → 1234.
Edge: "12,50" → 2 digits → decimal → 12.5. Good (EU).
Edge: "0,00" → decimal.
Edge: "-" only → null.
Edge: "" → null.
Edge: "(500.00)" → negative 500.
Edge: "500.00-" → negative.
Edge: "1.234.567,89" → both: last comma → decimal; remove dots → 1234567.89.
Edge: "1,234,567.89" → both → last dot → 1234567.89.
Edge: "1.234,56-" → negative.

Also `formatAmount(value)` → `Math.abs(value).toFixed(2)`.

Hoy `lib/parsers/csv-parser.ts` usa `file.text()` y por eso los CSV en CP1252/Latin-1 llegan con mojibake (`DescripciÃ³n`). El módulo debe correr tanto en el browser (se usa desde un client component) como en Node 20+/jsdom para los tests. **No importes nada de `node:*` ni agregues dependencias**; usá `TextDecoder` si existe y un fallback propio cuando no.\n\n## API exacta a implementar\n\n```ts\nexport interface DecodedText {\n  text: string\n  encoding: \"utf-8\" | \"utf-16le\" | \"utf-16be\" | \"windows-1252\"\n  hadBom: boolean\n}\n\n/** Decodifica bytes a string detectando BOM/UTF-8/CP1252. */\nexport const decodeBytes = (buffer: ArrayBuffer): DecodedText\n\n/** Limpieza y normalización de texto para parsing. */\nexport const normalizeText = (input: string): string\n\n/**\n * Si el texto arranca con una línea `sep=;` (formato que genera Excel al exportar),\n * la quita y devuelve el delimitador indicado.\n */\nexport const stripSepLine = (text: string): { text: string; delimiter: string | null }\n\n/** Heurística para sniffing: ¿el buffer parece texto plano (CSV/TXT) y no binario? */\nexport const looksLikeText = (buffer: ArrayBuffer): boolean\n```\n\n### `decodeBytes` — reglas\n- Detectar BOM en este orden: UTF-8 (`EF BB BF`), UTF-16LE (`FF FE`), UTF-16BE (`FE FF`). Si hay BOM, decodificar con ese encoding y `hadBom: true`, sin incluir el BOM en `text`.\n- Sin BOM: intentar UTF-8 estricto con `new TextDecoder(\"utf-8\", { fatal: true })`. Si lanza, decodificar como `windows-1252` (devolver `encoding: \"windows-1252\"`).\n- Si `TextDecoder` no existe en el entorno, implementar fallback manual: decodificador UTF-8 propio (validando secuencias; si es inválido, reinterpretar como CP1252) y decodificador CP1252 con tabla para el rango `0x80–0x9F` (identidad para el resto de bytes Latin-1).\n- Devolver el texto ya pasado por `normalizeText` (así todos los consumidores reciben texto limpio). `hadBom` refleja el BOM original.\n\n### `normalizeText` — reglas\n- `normalize(\"NFKC\")`.\n- Eliminar `\\uFEFF` (BOM/ZWNBSP), `\\u200B`, `\\u200C`, `\\u200D`.\n- `\\u00A0` (NBSP) → espacio; también `\\u202F`, `\\u2007`.\n- Unificar saltos de línea: `\\r\\n` y `\\r` → `\\n`.\n- Normalizar signos: `−` (U+2212), `–` (en dash), `—` (em dash) → `-`. También `’`/`‘` → `'` y `“`/`”` → `\"`.\n- Colapsar runs de espacios/tabs horizontales a un solo espacio.\n- Hacer `trim()` por línea y quitar líneas que quedaron vacías al final del texto (pero **conservar** las líneas vacías intermedias, porque separan bloques de tablas).\n- No alterar letras acentuadas ni mayúsculas (el matcheo de meses/cabeceras se hace en otros módulos).\n\n### `stripSepLine`\n- Solo mira las primeras líneas no vacías del texto ya normalizado.\n- Si una línea matchea `/^sep\\s*=\\s*(.)$/i` (un solo carácter no alfanumérico, típicamente `,` `;` `\\t` o `|`), devuelve `{ text: <texto sin esa línea>, delimiter: <carácter> }`.\n- Si no, devuelve `{ text, delimiter: null }`.\n\n### `looksLikeText`\n- Mira los primeros 4096 bytes. Devuelve `false` si hay bytes NUL (`0x00`), si hay BOM UTF-16, o si la proporción de bytes de control no imprimibles (excluyendo `\\t`, `\\n`, `\\r`) supera ~5%.\n- Devuelve `true` para archivos vacíos (se decidirá después).\n\n## Tests (`tests/unit/parsers/text.spec.ts`)\nUsá Vitest (`describe`/`it`/`expect`). Los tests de `tests/unit/**` corren en entorno **node**. Escribí al menos 14 casos, en español, con el estilo de los tests existentes del repo (comentarios `// Dado:` / `// Cuando:` / `// Entonces:`). Casos obligatorios:\n- UTF-8 con acentos decodifica bien.\n- UTF-8 con BOM quita el BOM y `hadBom: true`.\n- Bytes CP1252 reales (`0xF3` = `ó`, `0xED` = `í`) producen texto correcto y `encoding: \"windows-1252\"`; construí el `ArrayBuffer` a mano con `new Uint8Array([...]).buffer`, sin `Buffer`.\n- UTF-16LE con BOM (`FF FE`) decodifica correctamente y `hadBom: true`.\n- `normalizeText` convierte `−` y `–` a `-`, NBSP a espacio, CRLF a LF y colapsa espacios múltiples.\n- `normalizeText` conserva líneas vacías intermedias y elimina líneas vacías finales.\n- `stripSepLine` quita `sep=;` y devuelve `\";\"`; y devuelve `null` cuando no hay línea `sep=`.\n- `looksLikeText` devuelve `false` para un buffer con bytes NUL y `true` para texto CSV simple.\n- `decodeBytes` con buffer vacío no rompe.\n\n## Verificación obligatoria antes de terminar\n1. `npx tsc --noEmit` no debe reportar errores en tus dos archivos (ignorá errores preexistentes de otros archivos si los hay).\n2. `npx vitest run tests/unit/parsers/text.spec.ts --reporter=basic` debe pasar todos los tests.\n\nAl terminar respondé con: rutas creadas, resumen de decisiones (sobre todo el fallback de decodificación), y el resultado exacto de los tests. Si algo del spec es ambiguo, elegí la opción más simple y anotalo en la respuesta."
```ts
}
```

Separator logic as planned:
```
const hasDot = s.includes(".")
const hasComma = s.includes(",")
if (hasDot && hasComma) {
  dec = lastDot > lastComma ? "." : ","
  thousands = dec === "." ? "," : "."
  s = s.split(thousands).join("")
  if (dec === ",") s = s.replace(",", ".")
} else if (hasComma || hasDot) {
  const sep = hasComma ? "," : "."
  const parts = s.split(sep)
  if (parts.length > 2) { s = parts.join("") }  // 1.234.567 -> thousands
  else {
    const intPart = parts[0], frac = parts[1]
    if (frac.length === 3 && intPart.length >= 1 && intPart.length <= 3 && !opts.decimalSeparator) {
      // ambiguous 1.234 -> thousands (bank amounts rarely have 3 decimals)
      s = intPart + frac
    } else if (frac.length === 3 && opts.decimalSeparator === sep) {
      s = intPart + "." + frac
    } else if (frac.length === 0) {
      s = intPart
    } else {
      s = intPart + "." + frac
    }
  }
}
```

### dates.ts

Implement carefully.

```ts
export type DateConfidence = "high" | "medium" | "low"
export interface ParsedDate { iso: string; confidence: DateConfidence }
export interface ParseDateOptions {
  dayFirst?: boolean
  minYear?: number
  maxYear?: number
  /** Permite seriales de Excel (default true) */
  allowSerial?: boolean
}
```

`buildIso(y, m, d)`: validate integers, ranges, and real calendar via `const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m-1, d))` and compare getUTCFullYear/Month/Date. Also year 1900..2100 default.

Month names map (accent-stripped, lowercase):
es abbreviations: ene, feb, mar, abr, may, jun, jul, ago, sep, sept, set, oct, nov, dic.
es full: enero, febrero, marzo, abril, mayo, junio, julio, agosto, septiembre, setiembre, octubre, noviembre, diciembre.
en: jan, feb, mar, apr, may, jun, jul, aug, sep, sept, oct, nov, dec; january...december.

Normalization for month lookup: strip accents, lowercase, remove trailing ".".

Formats:
1. `/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})(?:[T\s].*)?$/` → y-m-d, high
2. `/^(\d{4})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})$/`
3. `/^(\d{4})-([A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚáéíóúñÑ]{3,})\.?-(\d{1,2})$/` → y-Mmm-d, high
4. `/^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})$/` → 8 digits, high (only if year 19xx/20xx)
5. `/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{2,4})$/` → ambiguous; resolve:
   - year: 2 digits → expand (>=70 → 1900+, else 2000+)
   - if a>12 && b<=12 → d=a, m=b (high)
   - if b>12 && a<=12 → m=a, d=b (high)
   - both <=12 → dayFirst ?? true (medium)
   - confidence depends.
6. `/^(\d{1,2})[\s\-]([A-Za-zÁÉ...]{3,})[\s\-,]+(\d{2,4})$/` → d Mmm y (high)
   Also "7 de septiembre de 2026": `/^(\d{1,2})\s+de\s+([A-Za-z]+)\s+de\s+(\d{2,4})$/i` → handle "de" by pre-stripping `\bde\b`.
7. `/^([A-Za-z]{3,})[\s\-]+(\d{1,2}),?[\s\-]+(\d{2,4})$/` → Mmm d y (high)
8. `/^(\d{1,2})-([A-Za-z]{3})-(\d{2,4})$/` covered by 6 with separator "-".

Scoring function `scoreHeaderCell(header, keys)`: normalize header (remove accents, lowercase, collapse non-alnum to space). For each key: if normalized === key → 100; if startsWith(key+" ") or endsWith(" "+key) → 70; if includes(key) → 50; also length penalty. Take max. Return score.

Then per role, pick the column with the highest score, with tie-break by column order. Must prevent the same column used by two roles (e.g., "saldo" matching amount). Implement greedy assignment in priority order: date, description, reference, balance, debit, credit, amount, type — but debit/credit/amount share; assign distinct columns. Also if a column scored for both debit and credit (e.g., "Débito/Crédito"), handle later (Phase 2).

`detectHeader(matrix)`: for each row index in 0..min(len, 40): compute mapping (needs at least date score>0 and (amount|debit|credit)>0). score = sum of role scores. Also require date score >= 50. Pick max score; tie → earliest. Return `{ rowIndex, score, mapping }`.

```
let amountValue: number | null = null
let type: TransactionType | null = null
const amountRaw = get(mapping.amount)
const debitRaw = get(mapping.debit)
const creditRaw = get(mapping.credit)
const typeRaw = get(mapping.type)

if (amountRaw) {
  const parsed = parseAmount(amountRaw, { currency })
  if (parsed.value === null) { errors.push(`Row ${n}: no valid amount found`); return }
  amountValue = Math.abs(parsed.value)
  if (typeRaw) type = resolveTypeFromColumn(typeRaw) ?? (parsed.negative ? "expense" : "income")
  else type = parsed.negative ? "expense" : "income"
} else if (debitRaw || creditRaw) {
  const debit = parseAmount(debitRaw).value
  const credit = parseAmount(creditRaw).value
  const d = debit === null ? 0 : Math.abs(debit)
  const c = credit === null ? 0 : Math.abs(credit)
  if (c > 0) { type="income"; amountValue=c }
  else if (d > 0) { type="expense"; amountValue=d }
  else { errors.push(`Row ${n}: no valid amount found`); return }  // change: zero → error instead of 0.00 txn
} else if (typeRaw) {
  // amount may be in a column not mapped? no
  errors.push(...)
}
```

```ts
export const parseCsv = async (file, fileName) => {
  let rawText = ""
  try {
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
    const decoded = decodeBytes(buffer)
    const { text } = stripSepLine(decoded.text)
    rawText = text
    const result = await new Promise<Papa.ParseResult<string[]>>((resolve, reject) => {
      Papa.parse<string[]>(text, {
        header: false,
        skipEmptyLines: "greedy",
        delimiter: sepDelimiter ?? "",
        complete: resolve,
        error: (err) => reject(err),
      })
    })
    const matrix = result.data.map(row => row.map(cell => String(cell ?? "")))
    const warnings = result.errors.map(e => `CSV line ${e.row != null ? e.row + 1 : "?"}: ${e.message}`)
    const parsed = parseTable(matrix, { fileName, source: "csv" })
    return { transactions, errors, warnings: [...warnings, ...parsed.warnings], source: "csv", fileName, rawText, detection: {...} }
  } catch (e) {
    return { transactions: [], errors: ["Could not read the CSV file"], source: "csv", fileName, rawText }
  }
}
```
