But the mock signature types: `((_source, config) => {...}) as typeof Papa.parse`. Works.

```ts
const workbook = XLSX.read(arrayBuffer, { type: "array" })
// choose best sheet
let best: { matrix, headerRow, score } | null
for sheetName of workbook.SheetNames:
  const ws = workbook.Sheets[sheetName]
  const json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<unknown[]>(ws, { header: 1, defval: "", raw: true, blankrows: false })
  const matrix = json.map(row => row.map(cell => String(cell ?? "")))
  const header = detectHeader(matrix)
  if header && (!best || header.score > best.score) best = {...}
if (!best) return error "No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel"
parseTable(best.matrix, { fileName, source: "excel", firstRowNumber: 1 })
rawText = best.matrix.map(r=>r.join("\t")).join("\n")
```

let dayVotes=0, monthVotes=0

  if (!m) continue

  if (a>12 && b<=12) dayVotes++
  else if (b>12 && a<=12) monthVotes++

if (monthVotes>0 && dayVotes===0) return false
return true

```ts
const normalizeSeparators = (digits: string): string => {
  const hasDot = digits.includes(".")
  const hasComma = digits.includes(",")
  if (hasDot && hasComma) {
    const lastDot = digits.lastIndexOf(".")
    const lastComma = digits.lastIndexOf(",")
    if (lastComma > lastDot) return digits.replace(/\./g, "").replace(",", ".")
    return digits.replace(/,/g, "")
  }
  if (!hasDot && !hasComma) return digits
  const sep = hasDot ? "." : ","
  const parts = digits.split(sep)
  if (parts.length > 2) return parts.join("")
  const [intPart, fracPart] = parts
  if (fracPart.length === 0) return intPart
  if (fracPart.length === 3 && intPart.length > 0 && intPart.length <= 3) return intPart + fracPart
  return `${intPart || "0"}.${fracPart}`
}
```

Rules ordered:
1. DOP: `/\bRD\s*\$|\bDOP\b|\bPESOS?\s+DOMINICANOS?\b/`
2. NIO: `/\bC\s*\$|\bNIO\b|\bCORDOBAS?\b/`
3. PEN: `/\bS\s*\/|\bPEN\b|\bSOLES?\b/` — careful `S/` could appear... fine.
4. MXN: `/\bMX\s*\$|\bMXN\b/`
5. BOB: `/\bBOB\b|\bBS\.?(?=\s*[\d(])/`
6. PYG: `/₲|\bPYG\b|\bGUARANI(?:ES)?\b/`
7. UYU: `/\bUYU\b|\$U\b/`
8. CRC: `/₡|\bCRC\b|\bCOLONES?\b/`
9. GTQ: `/\bGTQ\b|\bQUETZALES?\b|(?:^|[\s(])Q\.?(?=\s*[\d(])/`
10. EUR: `/€|\bEUR\b|\bEUROS?\b/`
11. USD: `/\bUS\s*\$|\bU\$S\b|\bUSD\b|\bDOLAR(?:ES)?\b|\$/`
12. HNL: `/\bHNL\b|\bLPS\b|\bLEMPIRAS?\b|(?:^|[\s(])L\.?(?=\s*[\d(])/`
13. COP: `/\bCOP\b/`
14. ARS: `/\bARS\b|\bAR\s*\$/`
15. CLP: `/\bCLP\b/`

value.match(/^(\\d{1,2})[-/.](\\d{1,2})[-/.](\\d{2,4})$/)\n  if (numeric) {\n    const first = Number(numeric[1])\n    const second = Number(numeric[2])\n    const year = expandYear(Number(numeric[3]))\n\n    if (first > 12 && second <= 12) {\n      const iso = buildIso(year, second, first, options)\n      return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"high\" } : null\n    }\n\n    if (second > 12 && first <= 12) {\n      const iso = buildIso(year, first, second, options)\n      return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"high\" } : null\n    }\n\n    const dayFirst = options.dayFirst ?? true\n    const iso = dayFirst\n      ? buildIso(year, second, first, options)\n      : buildIso(year, first, second, options)\n\n    return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"medium\" } : null\n  }\n\n  // 20260907 (YYYYMMDD) o 07092026 (DDMMYYYY)\n  if (/^\\d{8}$/.test(value)) {\n    const firstFour = Number(value.slice(0, 4))\n    if (firstFour >= 1900 && firstFour <= 2100) {\n      const iso = buildIso(\n        firstFour,\n        Number(value.slice(4, 6)),\n        Number(value.slice(6, 8)),\n        options\n      )\n      return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"high\" } : null\n    }\n\n    const lastFour = Number(value.slice(4, 8))\n    if (lastFour >= 1900 && lastFour <= 2100) {\n      const iso = buildIso(\n        lastFour,\n        Number(value.slice(2, 4)),\n        Number(value.slice(0, 2)),\n        options\n      )\n      return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"low\" } : null\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Timestamps epoch (segundos o milisegundos).\n  if (/^\\d{13}$/.test(value)) {\n    const iso = epochToIso(Number(value), options)\n    return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"low\" } : null\n  }\n\n  if (/^\\d{10}$/.test(value)) {\n    const iso = epochToIso(Number(value) * 1000, options)\n    return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"low\" } : null\n  }\n\n  // Serial de Excel (5 dígitos, típicamente 20000..80000).\n  if (/^\\d{5,6}$/.test(value)) {\n    const serial = Number(value)\n    const iso = serialToIso(serial, options)\n    return iso ? { iso, confidence: \"low\" } : null\n  }\n\n  return null\n}\n\n/**\n * Decide el orden día/mes observando todas las fechas numéricas del archivo.\n * Si aparece algún primer número mayor a 12, el archivo es día/mes; si aparece\n * un segundo número mayor a 12, es mes/día. Sin evidencia se asume día/mes.\n */\nexport const resolveDayFirst = (samples: readonly string[]): boolean => {\n  let dayFirstVotes = 0\n  let monthFirstVotes = 0\n\n  for (const sample of samples) {\n    if (!sample) continue\n    const match = String(sample)\n      .trim()\n      .match(/^(\\d{1,2})[-/.](\\d{1,2})[-/.](\\d{2,4})$/)\n    if (!match) continue\n\n    const first = Number(match[1])\n    const second = Number(match[2])\n\n    if (first > 12 && second <= 12) dayFirstVotes += 1\n    else if (second > 12 && first <= 12) monthFirstVotes += 1\n  }\n\n  if (dayFirstVotes > 0 && monthFirstVotes === 0) return true\n  if (monthFirstVotes > 0 && dayFirstVotes === 0) return false\n  return true\n}\n\n/** `true` si `iso` es una fecha `YYYY-MM-DD` que existe en el calendario. */\nexport const isValidIsoDate = (iso: string): boolean => {\n  const match = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/.exec(iso ?? \"\")\n  if (!match) return false\n  return (\n    buildIso(Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]), Number(match[3]), {}) === iso\n  )\n}\n",

```ts
import * as XLSX from "xlsx"
import { detectHeader, parseTable, toMatrix } from "./core/table"
export const parseExcel = async (file, fileName) => {
  try {
    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
    const workbook = XLSX.read(arrayBuffer, { type: "array" })
    let best: { sheetName: string; matrix: Matrix; score: number } | null = null
    for (const sheetName of workbook.SheetNames) {
      const worksheet = workbook.Sheets[sheetName]
      if (!worksheet) continue
      const json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<unknown[]>(worksheet, { header: 1, defval: "", raw: true, blankrows: false })
      const matrix = toMatrix(json)
      if (matrix.length === 0) continue
      const detection = detectHeader(matrix)
      if (!detection) continue
      if (!best || detection.score > best.score) best = { sheetName, matrix, score: detection.score }
    }
    if (!best) return { transactions: [], errors: ["No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel"], source: "excel", fileName, rawText: "" }
    const parsed = parseTable(best.matrix, { fileName, source: "excel", noHeaderMessage: "..." })
    return { ...parsed shape, rawText: best.matrix.map(r=>r.join("\t")).join("\n") }
  } catch { ... "Could not read the Excel file" }
}
```

`scoreHeader`:
```ts
const scoreHeader = (header: string, keys: readonly HeaderKey[]): number => {
  const normalized = normalizeHeader(header)
  if (!normalized) return 0
  let best = 0
  for (const { key, weight = 1, exactOnly = false } of keys) {
    if (normalized === key) best = Math.max(best, 100 * weight)
    else if (!exactOnly && (normalized.startsWith(`${key} `) || normalized.endsWith(` ${key}`))) best = Math.max(best, 70 * weight)
    else if (!exactOnly && normalized.includes(key)) best = Math.max(best, 45 * weight)
  }
  return best
}
```
Careful: `key` may contain spaces ("fecha de operacion") and normalized header equals. Good.

```ts
const pickBest = (
  scores: Array<Partial<Record<ColumnRole, number>>>,
  role: ColumnRole,
  used: Set<number>,
  headers: string[],
  options: { excludeBalanceLike?: boolean } = {}
): number | null => {
  let bestIdx: number | null = null
  let bestScore = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < scores.length; i++) {
    if (used.has(i)) continue
    const score = scores[i][role] ?? 0
    if (score <= 0) continue
    if (options.excludeBalanceLike) {
      const h = normalizeHeader(headers[i] ?? "")
      if (h.includes("saldo") || h.includes("balance")) continue
    }
    if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestIdx = i }
  }
  return bestIdx
}
```

  if (parsed.value === null) { errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`); return }
  if (parsed.value === 0) { errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: amount is zero`); return }
  amountValue = Math.abs(parsed.value)
  const typeFromColumn = resolveTypeFromLabel(cell(mapping.type))
  type = typeFromColumn ?? (parsed.value < 0 ? "expense" : "income")
} else if (mapping.debit !== null || mapping.credit !== null) {
  const debit = parseAmount(cell(mapping.debit)).value
  const credit = parseAmount(cell(mapping.credit)).value
  const debitAbs = debit === null ? 0 : Math.abs(debit)
  const creditAbs = credit === null ? 0 : Math.abs(credit)
  if (creditAbs > 0) { type = "income"; amountValue = creditAbs }
  else if (debitAbs > 0) { type = "expense"; amountValue = debitAbs }
  else { errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`); return }
} else {
  errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`); return
}
```

Ascending: for i in 1..n-1: expected = rows[i-1].balance + rows[i].signed; mismatch if |expected - rows[i].balance| > 0.01.
Descending: for i in 0..n-2: expected = rows[i+1].balance + rows[i].signed; mismatch if |expected - rows[i].balance| > 0.01.

Then choose fewer mismatches; if 0 → checked true, no warnings. If both > 0 and equal → warn about the first mismatch (ascending). Limit messages.

null,\n  reference: null,\n  debit: null,\n  credit: null,\n  amount: null,\n  balance: null,\n  type: null,\n})\n\nconst buildMapping = (\n  headers: readonly string[]\n): { mapping: ColumnMapping; score: number } | null => {\n  const scores = scoreHeaders(headers)\n  if (scores.length === 0) return null\n\n  const mapping = emptyMapping()\n  const used = new Set<number>()\n  const noUsed = new Set<number>()\n\n  // Una columna única tipo \"Débito/Crédito\" se puntúa para ambos roles; en ese\n  // caso se interpreta como un monto firmado, no como dos columnas.\n  const debitTop = pickBest(headers, scores, \"debit\", noUsed)\n  const creditTop = pickBest(headers, scores, \"credit\", noUsed)\n  const amountTop = pickBest(headers, scores, \"amount\", noUsed, {\n    excludeBalanceLike: true,\n  })\n\n  if (debitTop !== null && debitTop === creditTop) {\n    used.add(debitTop)\n    mapping.amount = amountTop ?? debitTop\n  }\n\n  for (const role of COLUMN_ROLES) {\n    if (mapping[role] !== null) continue\n    const index = pickBest(headers, scores, role, used, {\n      excludeBalanceLike: role === \"amount\",\n    })\n    mapping[role] = index\n    if (index !== null) used.add(index)\n  }\n\n  if (mapping.date === null) return null\n  if (\n    mapping.amount === null &&\n    mapping.debit === null &&\n    mapping.credit === null\n  ) {\n    return null\n  }\n\n  const score = COLUMN_ROLES.reduce((total, role) => {\n    const index = mapping[role]\n    if (index === null) return total\n    return total + (scores[index][role] ?? 0) * ROLE_WEIGHT[role]\n  }, 0)\n\n  return { mapping, score }\n}\n\n/** Mapea cabeceras a columnas. Devuelve `null` si no hay fecha o monto. */\nexport const mapColumns = (headers: readonly string[]): ColumnMapping | null =>\n  buildMapping(headers)?.mapping ?? null\n\nconst isMostlyNumeric = (row: readonly string[]): boolean => {\n  const filled = row.filter((cell) => normalizeCell(cell) !== \"\")\n  if (filled.length === 0) return true\n  return filled.every((cell) => /^[\\d.,\\-+()$€₡\\s]+$/.test(normalizeCell(cell)))\n}\n\nconst columnValues = (\n  matrix: Matrix,\n  column: number,\n  from: number,\n  limit = 6\n): string[] => {\n  const values: string[] = []\n  for (let index = from; index < matrix.length && values.length < limit; index += 1) {\n    const value = normalizeCell(matrix[index]?.[column])\n    if (value) values.push(value)\n  }\n  return values\n}\n\n/**\n * Valida que la fila candidata sea realmente una cabecera comprobando que la\n * columna de fecha tenga datos parseables debajo.\n */\nconst validateCandidate = (\n  matrix: Matrix,\n  rowIndex: number,\n  mapping: ColumnMapping\n): number => {\n  if (mapping.date === null) return -1\n\n  const values = columnValues(matrix, mapping.date, rowIndex + 1)\n  if (values.length === 0) return 0\n\n  const parsed = values.filter((value) => parseDate(value) !== null).length\n  if (values.length >= 2 && parsed === 0) return -1\n\n  return values.length > 0 ? parsed / values.length : 0\n}\n\n/**\n * Encuentra la fila de cabecera con mejor puntuación.\n *\n * Se escanean las primeras `scanLimit` filas porque los bancos exportan\n * preámbulos (nombre del banco, cuenta, período, líneas vacías).\n */\nexport const detectHeader = (\n  matrix: Matrix,\n  options: { scanLimit?: number } = {}\n): HeaderDetection | null => {\n  const scanLimit = options.scanLimit ?? 40\n  let best: HeaderDetection | null = null\n  let bestScore = 0\n\n  const limit = Math.min(matrix.length, scanLimit)\n  for (let rowIndex = 0; rowIndex < limit; rowIndex += 1) {\n    const row = matrix[rowIndex] ?? []\n    if (isMostlyNumeric(row)) continue\n\n    const result = buildMapping(row)\n    if (!result) continue\n\n    const dateValidation = validateCandidate(matrix, rowIndex, result.mapping)\n    if (dateValidation < 0) continue\n\n    const score = result.score * (1 + dateValidation)\n    if (score > bestScore) {\n      bestScore = score\n      best = { rowIndex, score, mapping: result.mapping }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return best\n}\n\nconst INCOME_LABELS = [\n  \"credito\",\n  \"credit\",\n  \"abono\",\n  \"deposito\",\n  \"ingreso\",\n  \"entrada\",\n  \"haber\",\n  \"recibido\",\n  \"recibida\",\n  \"c\",\n  \"cr\",\n]\n\nconst EXPENSE_LABELS = [\n  \"debito\",\n  \"debit\",\n  \"cargo\",\n  \"retiro\",\n  \"egreso\",\n  \"salida\",\n  \"debe\",\n  \"enviado\",\n  \"enviada\",\n  \"d\",\n  \"db\",\n  \"dr\",\n]\n\n/** Interpreta una columna \"Tipo\"/\"D\"/\"C\" cuando existe. */\nexport const resolveTypeFromLabel = (\n  label: string\n): TransactionType | null => {\n  const normalized = stripAccents(label).toLowerCase().trim()\n  if (!normalized) return null\n\n  if (INCOME_LABELS.includes(normalized)) return \"income\"\n  if (EXPENSE_LABELS.includes(normalized)) return \"expense\"\n\n  if (/\\bcredito\\b|\\bcredit\\b|\\babono\\b|\\bdeposito\\b|\\bingreso\\b/.test(normalized)) {\n    return \"income\"\n  }\n  if (/\\bdebito\\b|\\bdebit\\b|\\bcargo\\b|\\bretiro\\b|\\begreso\\b/.test(normalized)) {\n    return \"expense\"\n  }\n\n  return null\n}\n\ninterface BalanceRow {\n  row: number\n  balance: number\n  /** Monto firmado: positivo para ingresos, negativo para gastos. */\n  signed: number\n}\n\n/**\n * Comprueba el saldo corrido en ambos sentidos (extractos ascendentes y\n * descendentes). Un resultado sin diferencias confirma que las columnas\n * débito/crédito están bien asignadas.\n */\nexport const reconcileBalances = (\n  rows: readonly BalanceRow[],\n  maxWarnings = 5\n): { checked: boolean; warnings: string[] } => {\n  if (rows.length < 2) return { checked: false, warnings: [] }\n\n  const tolerance = 0.01\n  const mismatchesFor = (descending: boolean): number[] => {\n    const indexes: number[] = []\n    const pairs = descending ? rows.length - 1 : rows.length - 1\n\n    for (let offset = 0; offset < pairs; offset += 1) {\n      const currentIndex = descending ? offset : offset + 1\n      const previousIndex = descending ? offset + 1 : offset\n      const current = rows[currentIndex]\n      const previous = rows[previousIndex]\n\n      const expected = previous.balance + current.signed\n      if (Math.abs(expected - current.balance) > tolerance) {\n        indexes.push(currentIndex)\n      }\n    }\n\n    return indexes\n  }\n\n  const ascending = mismatchesFor(false)\n  const descending = mismatchesFor(true)\n  const mismatches =\n    descending.length < ascending.length ? descending : ascending\n\n  if (mismatches.length === 0) {\n    return { checked: true, warnings: [] }\n  }\n\n  const warnings = mismatches.slice(0, maxWarnings).map((index) => {\n    const current = rows[index]\n    const previous = rows[index - 1] ?? rows[index + 1]\n    const expected = previous.balance + current.signed\n    return `Row ${current.row}: balance mismatch (expected ${expected.toFixed(\n      2\n    )}, found ${current.balance.toFixed(2)})`\n  })\n\n  if (mismatches.length > maxWarnings) {\n    warnings.push(\n      `...and ${mismatches.length - maxWarnings} more balance mismatches`\n    )\n  }\n\n  return { checked: true, warnings }\n}\n\n/**\n * Convierte una matriz tabular en transacciones.\n *\n * Reglas de monto:\n * - Si existe columna `amount`, el signo decide el tipo (salvo columna `type`).\n * - Si existen `debit`/`credit`, el que tenga valor decide el tipo.\n * - Montos en cero o ilegibles se reportan como error y no se convierten en\n *   transacciones de `0.00`.\n */\nexport const parseTable = (\n  matrix: Matrix,\n  options: TableParseOptions\n): TableParseResult => {\n  const firstRowNumber = options.firstRowNumber ?? 1\n  const empty: TableParseResult = {\n    transactions: [],\n    errors: [],\n    warnings: [],\n    mapping: null,\n    headerRow: null,\n    dayFirst: options.dayFirst ?? true,\n    balanceChecked: false,\n    currency: options.currency ?? null,\n  }\n\n  const detection = detectHeader(matrix)\n  if (!detection) {\n    return {\n      ...empty,\n      errors: [\n        options.noHeaderMessage ??\n          \"No se encontró una fila de cabecera válida en el archivo\",\n      ],\n    }\n  }\n\n  const { mapping } = detection\n  const headerRow = detection.rowIndex\n  const dataRows = matrix.slice(headerRow + 1)\n\n  const getCell = (row: readonly string[], index: number | null): string =>\n    index === null ? \"\" : normalizeCell(row[index])\n\n  // Resolver día/mes con evidencia de todo el archivo.\n  const dateSamples =\n    mapping.date === null\n      ? []\n      : columnValues(matrix, mapping.date, headerRow + 1, 200)\n  const dayFirst = options.dayFirst ?? resolveDayFirst(dateSamples)\n\n  const transactions: ParsedTransaction[] = []\n  const errors: string[] = []\n  const warnings: string[] = []\n  const balanceRows: BalanceRow[] = []\n  let currency = options.currency ?? null\n  let truncatedWarnings = 0\n\n  dataRows.forEach((row, index) => {\n    const absoluteRow = firstRowNumber + headerRow + 1 + index\n\n    if (!row.some((cell) => normalizeCell(cell) !== \"\")) return\n\n    const dateRaw = getCell(row, mapping.date)\n    const parsedDate = parseDate(dateRaw, { dayFirst })\n    if (!parsedDate) {\n      errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: invalid date (\"${dateRaw}\")`)\n      return\n    }\n\n    const amountRaw = getCell(row, mapping.amount)\n    const debitRaw = getCell(row, mapping.debit)\n    const creditRaw = getCell(row, mapping.credit)\n    const typeRaw = getCell(row, mapping.type)\n\n    let amountValue: number | null = null\n    let transactionType: TransactionType | null = null\n\n    if (amountRaw) {\n      const parsed = parseAmount(amountRaw, { currency })\n      if (parsed.value === null) {\n        errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`)\n        return\n      }\n      if (parsed.value === 0) {\n        errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: amount is zero`)\n        return\n      }\n\n      amountValue = Math.abs(parsed.value)\n      transactionType =\n        resolveTypeFromLabel(typeRaw) ??\n        (parsed.value < 0 ? \"expense\" : \"income\")\n      currency = currency ?? parsed.currency\n    } else if (debitRaw || creditRaw) {\n      const debit = Math.abs(parseAmount(debitRaw, { currency }).value ?? 0)\n      const credit = Math.abs(parseAmount(creditRaw, { currency }).value ?? 0)\n\n      if (credit > 0) {\n        transactionType = \"income\"\n        amountValue = credit\n      } else if (debit > 0) {\n        transactionType = \"expense\"\n        amountValue = debit\n      } else {\n        errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`)\n        return\n      }\n    } else {\n      errors.push(`Row ${absoluteRow}: no valid amount found`)\n      return\n    }\n\n    let description = getCell(row, mapping.description) || \"No description\"\n    if (description.length > 255) {\n      description = description.slice(0, 255)\n      truncatedWarnings += 1\n    }\n\n    const reference = getCell(row, mapping.reference) || null\n    const finalType: TransactionType = transactionType ?? \"expense\"\n\n    transactions.push({\n      tempId: `${options.fileName}-${absoluteRow}-${transactions.length}`,\n      bankReference: reference,\n      transactionDate: parsedDate.iso,\n      transactionDescription: description,\n      transactionType: finalType,\n      amount: amountToPositiveString(amountValue),\n      row: absoluteRow,\n    })\n\n    const balance = parseAmount(getCell(row, mapping.balance)).value\n    if (balance !== null) {\n      balanceRows.push({\n        row: absoluteRow,\n        balance,\n        signed: finalType === \"income\" ? (amountValue ?? 0) : -(amountValue ?? 0),\n      })\n    }\n  })\n\n  if (truncatedWarnings > 0) {\n    warnings.push(\n      `${truncatedWarnings} description${\n        truncatedWarnings === 1 ? \" was\" : \"s were\"\n      } truncated to 255 characters`\n    )\n  }\n\n  const reconciliation = reconcileBalances(\n    balanceRows,\n    options.maxBalanceWarnings ?? 5\n  )\n  warnings.push(...reconciliation.warnings)\n\n  return {\n    transactions,\n    errors,\n    warnings,\n    mapping,\n    headerRow: headerRow + 1,\n    dayFirst,\n    balanceChecked: reconciliation.checked,\n    currency: currency ?? null,\n  }\n}\n",
  "path": "D:\\Proyectos de Programación\\financial-control\\lib\\parsers\\core\\table.ts"
}

```ts
import Papa from "papaparse"
import { decodeBytes, stripSepLine } from "./core/text"
import { parseAmount, formatAmount } from "./core/money"
import { parseDate } from "./core/dates"
import { normalizeCell, parseTable } from "./core/table"
import type { ExtractResult } from "./types"

export const cleanAmount = (raw: string): string => formatAmount(parseAmount(raw).value)
export const normalizeDate = (raw: string): string | null => parseDate(raw)?.iso ?? null

export const parseCsv = async (file, fileName): Promise<ExtractResult> => {
  let rawText = ""
  try {
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
    const decoded = decodeBytes(buffer)
    const { text, delimiter } = stripSepLine(decoded.text)
    rawText = text

    const parsed = await new Promise<Papa.ParseResult<string[]>>((resolve, reject) => {
      Papa.parse<string[]>(text, {
        header: false,
        skipEmptyLines: "greedy",
        delimiter: delimiter ?? "",
        complete: (result) => resolve(result),
        error: (error) => reject(error),
      })
    })

    const matrix = parsed.data.map((row) =>
      Array.isArray(row) ? row.map(normalizeCell) : [normalizeCell(row)]
    )

    const table = parseTable(matrix, {
      fileName,
      source: "csv",
      noHeaderMessage: "No se encontró una fila de cabecera válida en el archivo CSV",
    })

    const parserWarnings = parsed.errors.map((error) => {
      const row = typeof error.row === "number" ? ` ${error.row + 1}` : ""
      return `CSV row${row}: ${error.message}`
    })

    return {
      transactions: table.transactions,
      errors: table.errors,
      warnings: [...parserWarnings, ...table.warnings],
      source: "csv",
      fileName,
      rawText: text,
      detection: {
        format: "csv",
        bank: null,
        currency: table.currency,
        confidence: table.mapping ? 0.9 : 0,
        headerRow: table.headerRow,
        delimiter: delimiter ?? null,
        encoding: decoded.encoding,
        sheetName: null,
        columns: toColumnIndexes(table.mapping),
        balanceChecked: table.balanceChecked,
      },
    }
  } catch {
    return { transactions: [], errors: ["Could not read the CSV file"], source: "csv", fileName, rawText }
  }
}
```

Excel:

```ts
import * as XLSX from "xlsx"
import { detectHeader, normalizeCell, parseTable, toMatrix, mappingToColumnIndexes } from "./core/table"
import type { ExtractResult } from "./types"

export const parseExcel = async (file, fileName) => {
  try {
    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
    const workbook = XLSX.read(arrayBuffer, { type: "array" })

    let best: { sheetName: string; matrix: Matrix; score: number } | null = null
    for (const sheetName of workbook.SheetNames) {
      const sheet = workbook.Sheets[sheetName]
      if (!sheet) continue
      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<unknown[]>(sheet, {
        header: 1,
        defval: "",
        raw: true,
        blankrows: false,
      })
      const matrix = toMatrix(rows as unknown[][])
      if (matrix.length === 0) continue
      const detection = detectHeader(matrix)
      if (!detection) continue
      if (!best || detection.score > best.score) best = { sheetName, matrix, score: detection.score }
    }

    if (!best) return { transactions: [], errors: ["No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel"], source: "excel", fileName, rawText: "" }

    const table = parseTable(best.matrix, {
      fileName,
      source: "excel",
      noHeaderMessage: "No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel",
    })

    const rawText = best.matrix.map((row) => row.join("\t")).join("\n")

    return {
      transactions: table.transactions,
      errors: table.errors,
      warnings: table.warnings,
      source: "excel",
      fileName,
      rawText,
      detection: { ... }
    }
  } catch {
    return { transactions: [], errors: ["Could not read the Excel file"], source: "excel", fileName, rawText: "" }
  }
}
```

Algunos bancos exportan el estado de cuenta en la segunda hoja\n * o incluyen una hoja de resumen antes.\n */\nexport const parseExcel = async (\n  file: File,\n  fileName: string\n): Promise<ExtractResult> => {\n  try {\n    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()\n    const workbook = XLSX.read(arrayBuffer, { type: \"array\" })\n\n    let best: SheetCandidate | null = null\n\n    for (const sheetName of workbook.SheetNames) {\n      const worksheet = workbook.Sheets[sheetName]\n      if (!worksheet) continue\n\n      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<unknown[]>(worksheet, {\n        header: 1,\n        defval: \"\",\n        raw: true,\n        blankrows: false,\n      })\n      const matrix = toMatrix(rows as unknown[][])\n      if (matrix.length === 0) continue\n\n      const detection = detectHeader(matrix)\n      if (!detection) continue\n\n      if (!best || detection.score > best.score) {\n        best = { sheetName, matrix, score: detection.score }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (!best) {\n      return {\n        transactions: [],\n        errors: [\"No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel\"],\n        source: \"excel\",\n        fileName,\n        rawText: \"\",\n      }\n    }\n\n    const table = parseTable(best.matrix, {\n      fileName,\n      source: \"excel\",\n      noHeaderMessage:\n        \"No se encontró la fila de cabecera en el archivo Excel\",\n    })\n\n    const rawText = best.matrix.map((row) => row.join(\"\\t\")).join(\"\\n\")\n\n    return {\n      transactions: table.transactions,\n      errors: table.errors,\n      warnings: table.warnings,\n      source: \"excel\",\n      fileName,\n      rawText,\n      detection: {\n        format: \"excel\",\n        bank: null,\n        currency: table.currency,\n        confidence: table.mapping ? 0.9 : 0,\n        headerRow: table.headerRow,\n        delimiter: null,\n        encoding: null,\n        sheetName: best.sheetName,\n        columns: mappingToColumnIndexes(table.mapping),\n        balanceChecked: table.balanceChecked,\n      },\n    }\n  } catch {\n    return {\n      transactions: [],\n      errors: [\"Could not read the Excel file\"],\n      source: \"excel\",\n      fileName,\n      rawText: \"\",\n    }\n  }\n}\n",
  "path": "D:\\Proyectos de Programación\\financial-control\\lib\\parsers\\excel-parser.ts"
}

DD/MM/YYYY   Referencia   Descripción   L débito   L crédito   L balance\n// Usamos matchAll global porque pdf.js no preserva saltos de línea limpios entre filas\nconst FORMATO_1_REGEX =\n  /(\\d{1,2}\\/\\d{1,2}\\/\\d{4})\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(.+?)\\s+L\\s*([\\d.,]+)\\s+L\\s*([\\d.,]+)\\s+L\\s*([\\d.,]+)/g\n\n// Formato 2: YYYY-Mes-DD   Referencia   Descripción   débito   crédito   balance\nconst FORMATO_2_REGEX =\n  /(\\d{4}-[A-Za-z]{3}-\\d{1,2})\\s+(\\S+)\\s+(.+?)\\s+([\\d.,]+)\\s+([\\d.,]+)\\s+([\\d.,]+)/g\n\nconst parseMatch = (\n  dateRaw: string,\n  ref: string,\n  desc: string,\n  debitRaw: string,\n  creditRaw: string\n): ParsedTransaction | null => {\n  const date = normalizeDate(dateRaw)\n  if (!date) return null\n\n  const debit = Math.abs(parseAmount(debitRaw).value ?? 0)\n  const credit = Math.abs(parseAmount(creditRaw).value ?? 0)\n\n  let type: \"income\" | \"expense\" = \"expense\"\n  let amount = \"0.00\"\n\n  if (credit > 0) {\n    type = \"income\"\n    amount = amountToPositiveString(credit)\n  } else if (debit > 0) {\n    type = \"expense\"\n    amount = amountToPositiveString(debit)\n  } else {\n    return null\n transactions: ParsedTransaction[] = []\n  const errors: string[] = []\n  const seen = new Set<string>()\n\n  // Formato 1: fechas DD/MM/YYYY con montos \"L\"\n  for (const match of text.matchAll(FORMATO_1_REGEX)) {\n    const [, dateRaw, ref, desc, debitRaw, creditRaw] = match\n    const key = `${dateRaw}-${ref}-${debitRaw}-${creditRaw}`\n    if (seen.has(key)) continue\n    seen.add(key)\n\n    const parsed = parseMatch(dateRaw, ref, desc, debitRaw, creditRaw)\n    if (parsed) {\n      transactions.push({\n        ...parsed,\n        tempId: seen.add(key)\n\n    const parsed = parseMatch(dateRaw, ref, desc, debitRaw, creditRaw)\n    if (parsed) {\n      transactions.push({\n        ...parsed,\n        tempId: `${fileName}-f2-${parsed.tempId}`,\n        row: transactions.length + 1,\n      })\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (transactions.length === 0) {\n    errors.push(\"No valid movements detected in Banco Atlántida PDF\")\n  }\n\n  return {\n    transactions,\n    errors,\n    source: \"pdf\",\n    fileName,\n  }\n}\n",

Cabecera con acentos y mayúsculas: `[\"Fecha\",\"Descripción\",\"Débito\",\"Crédito\"]` mapea correctamente los 4 roles.\n4. Cabecera con sinónimos: `[\"Date\",\"Description\",\"Withdrawal\",\"Deposit\"]` → date/description/debit/credit.\n5. Cabecera con `Total` y `Saldo`: `[\"Fecha\",\"Descripción\",\"Total\",\"Saldo\"]` → `amount` apunta a \"Total\" (índice 2) y `balance` a \"Saldo\" (índice 3); **`amount` no puede apuntar a la columna Saldo**.\n6. Sin cabecera válida: `[[\"foo\",\"bar\"],[\"1\",\"2\"]]` → `detectHeader` devuelve `null` y `parseTable` devuelve `headerRow: null`, 0 transacciones y el mensaje de `noHeaderMessage` (probá pasando uno custom).\n7. Columna \"Tipo\" con `D`/`C` (y `Débito`/`Crédito`) gana sobre el signo del monto.\n14. Monto con formato latino `1.234,56` → 1234.56; con paréntesis `(1,234.56)` → expense.\n\n**Fechas y filas**\n15. Día/mes ambiguo en todo el archivo: columna con `13/02/2026` presente → las filas `01/02/2026` se interpretan día/mes; con `02/13/2026` presente → mes/día.\n16. Fecha inválida (`31/02/2026`) → error `Row N: invalid date (\"31/02/2026\")` y la fila se omite.\n17. `firstRowNumber: 10` desplaza la numeración: primera transacción con `row: 12` (cabecera en fila 11).\n18. Implementation:

```ts
import type { CurrencyCode } from "./money"
import { looksLikeText } from "./text"

export type FileFormat = "csv" | "excel" | "pdf" | "unknown"

const PDF_SIGNATURE = [0x25,0x50,0x44,0x46] // %PDF
const ZIP_SIGNATURE = [0x50,0x4b,0x03,0x04] // PK..
const OLE_SIGNATURE = [0xd0,0xcf,0x11,0xe0,0xa1,0xb1,0x1a,0xe1]

const startsWith = (buffer: ArrayBuffer, bytes: number[]): boolean => { ... }

export const sniffFormatFromBytes = (buffer: ArrayBuffer): FileFormat => {
  if (buffer.byteLength === 0) return "unknown"
  if (startsWith(buffer, PDF_SIGNATURE)) return "pdf"
  if (startsWith(buffer, ZIP_SIGNATURE)) return "excel"
  if (startsWith(buffer, OLE_SIGNATURE)) return "excel"
  if (looksLikeText(buffer)) {
    // HTML disfrazado de .xls
    const head = decodeAscii(buffer, 2048).toLowerCase()
    if (head.includes("<html") || head.includes("<table")) return "excel"
    return "csv"
  }
  return "unknown"
}

export const resolveFileFormat = (fileName: string, buffer?: ArrayBuffer): FileFormat
```
`resolveFileFormat`: sniff first; if "unknown" fall back to extension. But a PDF named .txt → sniff says pdf. Good. A CSV named .xls? sniff says csv → parseCsv. Fine.

`decodeAscii` simple: `String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(buffer.slice(0, limit)))`.

{
  "content": "/**\n * Detección de formato y banco.\n *\n * Dos niveles:\n * 1. `sniffFormatFromBytes`: mira las firmas del archivo (PDF, ZIP/XLSX, OLE,\n *    HTML disfrazado de .xls) y cae a heurística de texto. Sirve para archivos\n *    con extensión incorrecta o sin extensión.\n * 2. `detectBank` / `detectCurrencyFromText`: reconocen el banco y la moneda\n *    por firmas de texto en el contenido. La detección es solo informativa:\n *    los parsers nunca dependen de saber el banco para mapear columnas.\n */\n\nimport type { CurrencyCode } from \"./money\"\nimport { looksLikeText } from \"./text\"\n\nexport type FileFormat = \"csv\" | \"excel\" | \"pdf\" | \"unknown\"\n\nconst PDF_SIGNATURE = [0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46] // %PDF\nconst ZIP_SIGNATURE = [0x50, 0x4b, 0x03, 0x04] // PK\\x03\\x04\nconst OLE_SIGNATURE = [0xd0, 0xcf, 0x11, 0xe0, 0xa1, 0xb1, 0x1a, 0xe1]\n\nconst startsWithBytes = (buffer: ArrayBuffer, bytes: readonly number[]): boolean => {\n  if (buffer.byteLength < bytes.length) return false\n  const view = new Uint8Array(buffer, 0, bytes.length)\n  return bytes.every((byte, index) => view[index] === byte)\n}\n\nconst asciiHead = (buffer: ArrayBuffer, limit = 2048): string => {\n  const length = Math.min(buffer.byteLength, limit)\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer, 0, length)\n  let out = \"\"\n  for (const byte of bytes) {\n    out += byte < 0x80 ? String.fromCharCode(byte) : \" \"\n  }\n  return out.toLowerCase()\n}\n\n/**\n * Detecta el formato real mirando el contenido, no la extensión.\n * Los archivos de texto se tratan como `csv`: el motor de tablas elige el\n * delimitador (coma, punto y coma, tabulación o pipe).\n */\nexport const sniffFormatFromBytes = (buffer: ArrayBuffer): FileFormat => {\n  if (buffer.byteLength === 0) return \"unknown\"\n  if (startsWithBytes(buffer, PDF_SIGNATURE)) return \"pdf\"\n  if (startsWithBytes(buffer, ZIP_SIGNATURE)) return \"excel\"\n  if (startsWithBytes(buffer, OLE_SIGNATURE)) return \"excel\"\n\n  if (looksLikeText(buffer)) {\n    const head = asciiHead(buffer)\n    // Algunos bancos exportan HTML con extensión .xls.\n    if (head.includes(\"<html\") || head.includes(\"<table\") || head.includes(\"<tr\")) {\n      return \"excel\"\n    }\n    return \"csv\"\n  }\n\n  return \"unknown\"\n}\n\nconst extensionFormat = (fileName: string): FileFormat => {\n  const lower = fileName.toLowerCase()\n  if (lower.endsWith(\".pdf\")) return \"pdf\"\n  if (lower.endsWith(\".xlsx\") || lower.endsWith(\".xls\") || lower.endsWith(\".xlsm\")) {\n    return \"excel\"\n  }\n  if (lower.endsWith(\".csv\") || lower.endsWith(\".txt\") || lower.endsWith(\".tsv\")) {\n    return \"csv\"\n  }\n  return \"unknown\"\n}\n\n/**\n * Combina el sniffing por bytes con la extensión. El contenido manda cuando\n * es reconocible; la extensión solo se usa como respaldo.\n */\nexport const resolveFileFormat = (\n  fileName: string,\n  buffer?: ArrayBuffer\n): FileFormat => {\n  if (buffer && buffer.byteLength > 0) {\n    const sniffed = sniffFormatFromBytes(buffer)\n    if (sniffed !== \"unknown\") return sniffed\n  }\n  return extensionFormat(fileName)\n}\n\ninterface BankSignature {\n  name: string\n  pattern: RegExp\n}\n\nconst stripAccents = (value: string): string =>\n  value.normalize(\"NFD\").replace(/[\\u0300-\\u036f]/g, \"\")\n\n/**\n * Firmas de bancos de Honduras y la región. Se comparan contra el texto\n * normalizado (sin acentos, minúsculas). El orden importa: las firmas más\n * específicas van primero.\n */\nconst BANK_SIGNATURES: readonly BankSignature[] = [\n  {\n    name: \"BAC Credomatic\",\n    pattern: /bac\\s*credomatic|bac\\s*credito|bac\\s*credomatico/,\n  },\n  { name: \"Banco Atlántida\", pattern: /banco\\s+atlantida|bancatlan|atlantida/ },\n  { name: \"Banco Ficohsa\", pattern: /ficohsa|banco\\s+ficohsa/ },\n  { name: \"Banco del País\", pattern: /banco\\s+del\\s+pais|banpais/ },\n  { name: \"Banco Davivienda\", pattern: /davivienda/ },\n  {\n    name: \"Banco de Desarrollo Rural\",\n    pattern: /banrural|banco\\s+de\\s+desarrollo\\s+rural/,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"Banco Agrícola Mercantil\",\n    pattern: /banco\\s+agricola\\s+mercantil|\\bbam\\b/,\n  },\n  { name: \"Banco Lafise\", pattern: /lafise|banco\\s+lafise/ },\n  { name: \"Banco Promerica\", pattern: /promerica/ },\n  {\n    name: \"Banco de Occidente\",\n    pattern: /banco\\s+de\\s+occidente|banco\\s+occidente/,\n  },\n  { name: \"Banco Cuscatlán\", pattern: /cuscatlan/ },\n]\n\n/** Devuelve el nombre del banco detectado en el texto, o `null`. */\nexport const detectBank = (text: string): string | null => {\n  if (!text) return null\n  const normalized = stripAccents(text).toLowerCase()\n  for (const signature of BANK_SIGNATURES) {\n    if (signature.pattern.test(normalized)) return signature.name\n  }\n  return null\n}\n\nconst CURRENCY_HINTS: ReadonlyArray<{ code: CurrencyCode; pattern: RegExp }> = [\n  { code: \"HNL\", pattern: /\\bhnl\\b|\\blempiras?\\b|\\blps\\b|\\bl\\.?(?=\\s*\\d)/g },\n  { code: \"USD\", pattern: /\\busd\\b|\\bdolares?\\b|\\bus\\s*\\$|\\$\\s*\\d/g },\n  { code: \"GTQ\", pattern: /\\bgtq\\b|\\bquetzales?\\b|\\bq\\.?(?=\\s*\\d)/g },\n  { code: \"CRC\", pattern: /\\bcrc\\b|\\bcolones?\\b|₡/g },\n  { code: \"NIO\", pattern: /\\bnio\\b|\\bcordobas?\\b/g },\n  { code: \"EUR\", pattern: /\\beur\\b|\\beuros?\\b|€/g },\n]\n\n/**\n * Infiere la moneda del archivo contando apariciones de su símbolo/código.\n * Devuelve `null` si no hay evidencia o si hay empate.\n */\nexport const detectCurrencyFromText = (text: string): CurrencyCode | null => {\n  if (!text) return null\n  const normalized = stripAccents(text).toLowerCase()\n\n  let best: CurrencyCode | null = null\n  let bestCount = 0\n  let tied = false\n\n  for (const hint of CURRENCY_HINTS) {\n    const matches = normalized.match(hint.pattern)\n    const count = matches?.length ?? 0\n    if (count === 0) continue\n\n    if (count > bestCount) {\n      best = hint.code\n      bestCount = count\n      tied = false\n    } else if (count === bestCount) {\n      tied = true\n    }\n  }\n\n  return tied ? null : best\n}\n",
  "path": "D:\\Proyectos de Programación\\financial-control\\lib\\parsers\\core\\detection.ts"
}

```ts
import type { DetectionInfo, ExtractResult, ParserSource } from "./types"
import { parseCsv } from "./csv-parser"
import { parseExcel } from "./excel-parser"
import { parseAtlántidaPDF } from "./atlantida-pdf-parser"
import { detectBank, detectCurrencyFromText, resolveFileFormat } from "./core/detection"

export const getFileSource = (fileName: string): ParserSource => {...}  // keep return type literal union

export const extractTransactions = async (file: File): Promise<ExtractResult> => {
  const fileName = file.name
  let source: ParserSource = getFileSource(fileName)

  try {
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
    source = resolveFileFormat(fileName, buffer)
  } catch {
    // si no se puede leer, seguimos con la extensión
  }

  if (source === "unknown") {
    return {
      transactions: [],
      errors: [`Incompatible file format: "${fileName}". Use .csv, .xlsx, .xls or .pdf`],
      source: "unknown",
      fileName,
    }
  }

  try {
    if (source === "csv") return await parseCsv(file, fileName)
    if (source === "excel") return await parseExcel(file, fileName)
    if (source === "pdf") return await extractPdfTransactions(file, fileName)
  } catch (error) {
    return { transactions: [], errors: [`Unexpected error processing "${fileName}": ${...}`], source, fileName }
  }

  return { transactions: [], errors: ["Unsupported file format"], source: "unknown", fileName }
}
```

const extractPdfTransactions = async (file, fileName): Promise<ExtractResult> => {
  const pdfjsLib = await import("pdfjs-dist")
  const worker = await import("pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs")
  if (pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc === undefined) {
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = (worker as any).default || worker
  }
  const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()
  const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer }).promise
  ...
  if (!fullText.trim()) return {...}
  const result = parseAtlántidaPDF(fullText, fileName)
  const detection: DetectionInfo = {
    format: "pdf",
    bank: detectBank(fullText),
    currency: detectCurrencyFromText(fullText),
    confidence: result.transactions.length > 0 ? 0.7 : 0,
    headerRow: null,
    delimiter: null,
    encoding: null,
    sheetName: null,
    columns: {},
    balanceChecked: false,
  }
  return { ...result, rawText: fullText, detection }

This reveals a real design issue: sniffing by content alone can misclassify. The rule should be: content sniffing only *overrides* the extension when it detects a known binary signature (PDF, ZIP, OLE) or when the extension is unknown/ambiguous. - Actually simplest and safest: 
  - sniffed === "pdf" or "excel" → return sniffed (strong binary signatures).
  - ext known → return ext.
  - sniffed === "csv" and ext === "unknown" → "csv".
  - ext unknown and sniff unknown → unknown.

- Only use text sniffing when ext is unknown **and** the file name has no recognized binary extension. That blocklist approach handles it.

```ts
export const resolveFileFormat = (fileName, buffer) => {
  const ext = extensionFormat(fileName)
  if (isUnsupportedExtension(fileName)) return "unknown"
  if (buffer && buffer.byteLength > 0) {
    const sniffed = sniffFormatFromBytes(buffer)
    if (sniffed === "pdf" || sniffed === "excel") return sniffed
    if (ext !== "unknown") return ext
    if (sniffed === "csv") return "csv"
  }
  return ext
}
```

Design details:

```ts
export interface PdfTextItem {
  str: string
  transform: number[]   // [a,b,c,d,e,f]
  width: number
  height: number
  hasEOL?: boolean
}

export interface PdfCell { text: string; x: number; xEnd: number }
export interface PdfRow { y: number; cells: PdfCell[] }

export interface LayoutOptions {
  /** Tolerancia vertical en puntos (default: 2 o 60% de la altura). */
  yTolerance?: number
  /** Hueco horizontal máximo para unir dos items en la misma celda. */
  mergeGap?: number
}

export const buildPdfRows = (items: readonly PdfTextItem[], options: LayoutOptions = {}): PdfRow[]
export const rowsToText = (rows: readonly PdfRow[]): string
```

Algorithm:
```
const entries = items.filter(i => i.str && i.str.trim() !== "").map(...)
// Some PDFs emit items with only spaces; keep them? They indicate gaps. Merge into previous cell? Keep spaces as separators.
const yTolerance = options.yTolerance ?? 2.5
// 1. group by y
const groups: { y: number; items: Entry[] }[] = []
for (const entry of entries) {
  const group = groups.find(g => Math.abs(g.y - entry.y) <= yTolerance)
  if (group) { group.items.push(entry); group.y = (group.y * ... )? }
  else groups.push({ y: entry.y, items: [entry] })
}
```
Using `find` is O(n^2) with many items; PDFs have maybe 1000-5000 items. 25M ops worst case — acceptable but could be slow. Better sort by y descending then group consecutive within tolerance. Sorting is O(n log n) and robust. But y jitter within a row (superscripts) — consecutive grouping with running mean handles it. Use:
```
entries.sort((a,b) => b.y - a.y || a.x - b.x)
for entry: if last group && Math.abs(lastGroup.y - entry.y) <= tolerance → push; else new group
```
Then within each group sort by x and merge.

Merge:
```
const cells: PdfCell[] = []
for (const item of group.items.sort by x) {
  const text = item.str
  const last = cells[cells.length-1]
  const gap = item.x - last.xEnd
  if (last && gap <= mergeGap) {
     const needsSpace = gap > spaceThreshold && !/\s$/.test(last.text) && !/^\s/.test(text)
     last.text += (needsSpace ? " " : "") + text
     last.xEnd = Math.max(last.xEnd, item.xEnd)
  } else {
     cells.push({ text, x: item.x, xEnd: item.xEnd })
  }
}
```
Where spaceThreshold ~ max(1, height*0.15). And mergeGap = max(3, height*0.6).

Hmm: items might include explicit space strings " " — `str.trim()===""` filtered out; but that loses the info that two words are separated. If we filter them, gap computed from x positions remains, so we can insert a space when gap > small threshold. Good.

But merging words within a cell: if gap between words is small (2pt) we merge with no space → "CompraSupermercado". Need space insertion for any gap > ~0.5pt. Since we filtered explicit spaces, insert a space when `gap > spaceThreshold` where spaceThreshold = max(0.5, height * 0.08). At 10pt font, 0.8pt. Word gap is ~2-3pt → space inserted ✓. Characters within a word are usually one item (pdf.js groups by font run), so internal gaps are 0.

Actually if a cell's text is split into multiple items due to font changes, gaps could be ~0.1pt → no space. Good.

Cell merge threshold: gap between adjacent columns ~10-30pt > mergeGap (6pt at 10pt font). Good. But a wide description column adjacent to a narrow reference column could be < 6pt? Unlikely.

So:
```
const spaceThreshold = Math.max(0.6, height * 0.08)
const mergeGap = Math.max(3, height * (options.mergeGapFactor ?? 0.7))
```
Use average height of group.

Edge: rotated text (transform b/c nonzero) — ignore; assume horizontal. Many statements are horizontal.

Also `hasEOL` could force a new cell? No, it marks end of line, but grouping by y handles it.

Implementation:

```ts
const isTextItem = (item: any): item is PdfTextItem => typeof item?.str === "string" && Array.isArray(item?.transform) && item.transform.length >= 6

export const buildPdfRows = (items, options = {}) => {
  const entries = items.filter(isTextItem).map(item => {
    const x = Number(item.transform[4])
    const y = Number(item.transform[5])
    const height = Math.abs(Number(item.height)) || 10
    const width = Math.abs(Number(item.width)) || 0
    return { text: item.str, x, y, xEnd: x + width, height }
  }).filter(e => Number.isFinite(e.x) && Number.isFinite(e.y) && e.text.trim() !== "")
  ...
}
```
Note: `width` can be 0 for some items; then xEnd = x → merging may collapse. Use `width` if >0 else estimate from str length * height * 0.5. Good: `const width = item.width > 0 ? item.width : text.length * height * 0.5`.

Grouping tolerance: use max(2, avgHeight*0.4)? If line spacing is 12pt and height 10, tolerance 4 is safe (less than half line spacing). Use `options.yTolerance ?? 3`.

Let me use 3 default; for large fonts 12pt height, 3 still fine.

Rows sorted top-down (descending y). Good.

Merging: per row, sort by x.

Cell text: `text.trim()` at the end; collapse spaces.

Now generic-pdf.ts.

```ts
import { mapColumns, parseTable, detectHeader } from "../core/table"
import { parseDate } from "../core/dates"
import { buildPdfRows, type PdfRow, type PdfTextItem } from "./layout"
import type { ExtractResult } from "../types"

export const parsePdfWithLayout = (pages: PdfTextItem[][], fileName: string): ExtractResult | null => {
  const rows = pages.flatMap(page => buildPdfRows(page))
  if (rows.length === 0) return null

  // 1. Localizar cabecera
  const headerSearch = rows.slice(0, 60)
  let headerIndex = -1
  let headerCells: PdfCell[] = []
  let headerMapping = null
  for (let i = 0; i < headerSearch.length; i++) {
    const cells = headerSearch[i].cells
    if (cells.length < 3) continue
    const mapping = mapColumns(cells.map(c => c.text))
    if (!mapping) continue
    // validar con la primera fila de datos? 
    headerIndex = i; headerCells = cells; headerMapping = mapping; break
  }
  if (headerIndex === -1 || !headerMapping) return null
```
Wait: mapColumns requires a date and amount column; a page header "Fecha Descripción Monto" would map. Good.

But `mapColumns` uses normalized texts; the cells must be separate. Fine.

2. Column assignment: for each header cell, center = (x+xEnd)/2. For each data row cell, choose the header cell minimizing |center - dataCenter|. But some cells might be a leading "-" or continuation. Also the description cell may span; nearest center works if description header center is near its data. If description is long, data cell center may be far right, closer to the amount column! That's a real risk: description text spanning x 100-400, description header at 120 center, amount header at 450 center → data cell center 250 → nearest is description (dist 130) vs amount (200) → description ✓. But if description spans 100-440, center 270 → still description. If description is very long up to 430, center 265. OK.

Alternative: assign by cell start x (left edge) to the nearest header left edge — descriptions start at their column's left. Numbers are right-aligned, so use right edge for numeric cells. Best: for each cell, decide if it looks numeric; if numeric → compare xEnd (right edge) to header xEnd; else compare x (left edge) to header x. That's much more accurate. Implement:
```
const numericLike = /^[-+(]?[\d.,\s$LQ€₡HNLUSD]+\)?-?$/i.test(text) && /\d/.test(text)
const anchor = numericLike ? cell.xEnd : cell.x
const headerAnchor = numericLike ? header.xEnd : header.x
pick min |anchor - headerAnchor|
```
Good.

3. Build matrix:
```
const width = headerCells.length
const matrix: string[][] = [headerCells.map(c => c.text)]
const dateCol = headerMapping.date
let lastDescriptionIndex = matrix.length - 1
for each row after header:
   const cells = new Array(width).fill("")
   for each cell: col = assign; cells[col] = cells[col] ? cells[col] + " " + text : text
   // skip repeated header rows
   if (isSameHeader(cells)) continue
   // skip empty
   if (cells.every(c => !c)) continue
   const dateValue = cells[dateCol]
   if (!dateValue || !parseDate(dateValue)) {
      // possible description continuation: if cells have only description content and previous row exists
      const nonEmpty = cells.map((v,i)=>[i,v]).filter(([,v])=>v)
      if (nonEmpty.length === 1 && nonEmpty[0][0] === descCol && matrix.length > 1) {
         // append to previous row description
         const prev = matrix[matrix.length-1]
         prev[descCol] = `${prev[descCol]} ${nonEmpty[0][1]}`.trim()
         continue
      }
      // summary/footer/page row → skip
      continue
   }
   matrix.push(cells)
```
Careful: reference column might be separate and cells with placeholder "0" etc. Fine.

{
  "content": "/**\n * Reconstrucción de la estructura de un PDF a partir de los `TextItem` de\n * pdf.js.\n *\n * pdf.js entrega cada fragmento de texto con su matriz de transformación\n * (`transform[4]` = X, `transform[5]` = Y), ancho y alto. Perder esa\n * información (como hacía `items.map(i => i.str).join(\" \")`) obliga a adivinar\n * las columnas con regex. Aquí se agrupan los fragmentos por línea (Y) y se\n * unen en celdas por proximidad horizontal (X), que es lo que necesita el\n * motor genérico de tablas.\n */\n\nexport interface PdfTextItem {\n  str: string\n  transform: number[]\n  width?: number\n  height?: number\n  hasEOL?: boolean\n}\n\nexport interface PdfCell {\n  text: string\n  /** Borde izquierdo de la celda, en puntos PDF. */\n  x: number\n  /** Borde derecho de la celda, en puntos PDF. */\n  xEnd: number\n}\n\nexport interface PdfRow {\n  /** Coordenada Y de la línea (las filas se devuelven de arriba hacia abajo). */\n  y: number\n  cells: PdfCell[]\n}\n\nexport interface PdfLayoutOptions {\n  /** Tolerancia vertical para considerar dos fragmentos de la misma línea. */\n  yTolerance?: number\n  /** Hueco horizontal máximo para unir fragmentos en una misma celda. */\n  mergeGap?: number\n}\n\ninterface PositionedItem {\n  text: string\n  x: number\n  xEnd: number\n  y: number\n  height: number\n}\n\nconst isTextItem = (item: unknown): item is PdfTextItem => {\n  if (!item || typeof item !== \"object\") return false\n  const candidate = item as PdfTextItem\n  return (\n    typeof candidate.str === \"string\" &&\n    Array.isArray(candidate.transform) &&\n    candidate.transform.length >= 6\n  )\n}\n\nconst positionItem = (item: PdfTextItem): PositionedItem | null => {\n  const text = item.str\n  if (!text || text.trim() === \"\") return null\n\n  const x = Number(item.transform[4])\n  const y = Number(item.transform[5])\n  if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) return null\n\n  const height = Math.abs(Number(item.height)) || 10\n  const measuredWidth = Math.abs(Number(item.width))\n  // Algunos generadores no informan `width`; se estima con el tamaño de fuente.\n  const width =\n    measuredWidth > 0 ? measuredWidth : text.length * height * 0.5\n\n  return { text, x, xEnd: x + width, y, height }\n}\n\n/**\n * Agrupa los fragmentos en filas (por Y) y los une en celdas (por X).\n *\n * - La tolerancia vertical por defecto es 3pt, suficiente para ignorar\n *   diferencias de baseline sin mezclar renglones.\n * - Dos fragmentos se unen en la misma celda si el hueco es menor a\n *   `max(3, altura * 0.7)`. Entre columnas el hueco es mucho mayor.\n * - Cuando el hueco supera el ancho estimado de un espacio, se inserta un\n *   espacio para no pegar palabras.\n */\nexport const buildPdfRows = (\n  items: readonly unknown[],\n  options: PdfLayoutOptions = {}\n): PdfRow[] => {\n  const yTolerance = options.yTolerance ?? 3\n\n  const positioned = items\n    .filter(isTextItem)\n    .map(positionItem)\n    .filter((item): item is PositionedItem => item !== null)\n\n  if (positioned.length === 0) return []\n\n  // Ordenar de arriba hacia abajo (Y decreciente) y de izquierda a derecha.\n  positioned.sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y || a.x - b.x)\n\n  const groups: { y: number; items: PositionedItem[] }[] = []\n  for (const item of positioned) {\n    const current = groups[groups.length - 1]\n    if (current && Math.abs(current.y - item.y) <= yTolerance) {\n      current.items.push(item)\n    } else {\n      groups.push({ y: item.y, items: [item] })\n    }\n  }\n\n  return groups.map((group) => {\n    const ordered = [...group.items].sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x)\n    const averageHeight =\n      ordered.reduce((total, item) => total + item.height, 0) / ordered.length\n\n    const mergeGap = options.mergeGap ?? Math.max(3, averageHeight * 0.7)\n    const spaceThreshold = Math.max(0.6, averageHeight * 0.08)\n\n    const cells: PdfCell[] = []\n\n    for (const item of ordered) {\n      const last = cells[cells.length - 1]\n      const gap = last ? item.x - last.xEnd : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY\n\n      if (last && gap <= mergeGap) {\n        const needsSpace =\n          gap > spaceThreshold &&\n          !/\\s$/.test(last.text) &&\n          !/^\\s/.test(item.text)\n        last.text = `${last.text}${needsSpace ? \" \" : \"\"}${item.text}`\n        last.xEnd = Math.max(last.xEnd, item.xEnd)\n      } else {\n        cells.push({ text: item.text, x: item.x, xEnd: item.xEnd })\n      }\n    }\n\n    return {\n      y: group.y,\n      cells: cells.map((cell) => ({\n        ...cell,\n        text: cell.text.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim(),\n      })),\n    }\n  })\n}\n\n/** Reconstruye un texto plano legible a partir de las filas detectadas. */\nexport const pdfRowsToText = (rows: readonly PdfRow[]): string =>\n  rows\n    .map((row) => row.cells.map((cell) => cell.text).join(\" \").trim())\n    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)\n    .join(\"\\n\")\n",
  "path": "D:\\Proyectos de Programación\\financial-control\\lib\\parsers\\pdf\\layout.ts"
}
```

  .replace(/\bL\.?(?=\s*[\d(])/gi, " ")

  let dist = Math.abs(anchor - headerAnchor)

})
return best

Build matrix:
```
const headerTexts = headerCells.map(c => c.text)
const width = headerCells.length
const matrix: Matrix = [headerTexts]
const mapping = mappingFromHeader
const dateCol = mapping.date!

for (let index = headerRowIndex + 1; index < rows.length; index++) {
  const row = rows[index]
  if (row.cells.length === 0) continue
  // repeated header?
  if (isRepeatedHeader(row.cells, headerTexts)) continue
  const cells = new Array(width).fill("")
  for (const cell of row.cells) {
    const col = assignCell(headerCells, cell)
    cells[col] = cells[col] ? `${cells[col]} ${cell.text}` : cell.text
  }
  if (cells.every(c => !c)) continue

  const dateValue = cells[dateCol] ?? ""
  if (!dateValue || !parseDate(dateValue)) {
    // continuación de descripción
    const nonEmpty = cells.map((v, i) => ({ i, v })).filter(x => x.v)
    const descCol = mapping.description
    if (descCol !== null && nonEmpty.length === 1 && nonEmpty[0].i === descCol && matrix.length > 1) {
      const previous = matrix[matrix.length - 1]
      previous[descCol] = `${previous[descCol]} ${nonEmpty[0].v}`.trim()
      continue
    }
    continue  // resumen/pie/página
  }

  matrix.push(cells)
  if (matrix.length > maxRows) break
}
if (matrix.length <= 1) return null
```

La matriz resultante entra en `parseTable`, así que hereda validación\n *      de fechas, montos, tipos, saldo y warnings.\n *\n * Si no se reconoce una cabecera se devuelve `null` y el llamador cae al\n * fallback por regex (`atlantida-pdf-parser.ts`).\n */\n\nimport { parseDate } from \"../core/dates\"\nimport { detectCurrencyFromText } from \"../core/detection\"\nimport {\n  mapColumns,\n  mappingToColumnIndexes,\n  parseTable,\n  type ColumnMapping,\n  type Matrix,\n} from \"../core/table\"\nimport {\n  buildPdfRows,\n  pdfRowsToText,\n  type PdfCell,\n  type PdfRow,\n} from \"./layout\"\nimport type { ExtractResult } from \"../types\"\n\nexport interface PdfLayoutParseOptions {\n  fileName: string\n  /** Banco detectado por firma de texto, solo informativo. */\n  bank?: string | null\n  /** Límite de filas para evitar documentos gigantes (default 5000). */\n  maxRows?: number\n  /** Filas a escanear buscando la cabecera (default 80). */\n  headerScanLimit?: number\n}\n\ninterface HeaderMatch {\n  rowIndex: number\n  cells: PdfCell[]\n  mapping: ColumnMapping\n}\n\nconst stripAccents = (value: string): string =>\n  value.normalize(\"NFD\").replace(/[\\u0300-\\u036f]/g, \"\")\n\nconst normalizeText = (value: string): string =>\n  stripAccents(value).toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim()\n\n/**\n * Heurística de alineación: si la celda tiene muchos dígitos y pocas letras\n * (montos con o sin símbolo de moneda) se asume alineada a la derecha.\n */\nconst looksNumeric = (text: string): boolean => {\n  const digits = text.match(/\\d/g)?.length ?? 0\n  const letters = text.match(/[a-záéíóúñ]/gi)?.length ?? 0\n  return digits > 0 && letters <= 3\n}\n\nconst anchorFor = (cell: PdfCell, numeric: boolean): number =>\n  numeric ? cell.xEnd : cell.x\n\n/** Asigna una celda a la columna de cabecera más cercana según su anclaje. */\nconst assignColumn = (headerCells: readonly PdfCell[], cell: PdfCell): number => {\n  const numeric = looksNumeric(cell.text)\n  const anchor = anchorFor(cell, numeric)\n\n  let bestIndex = 0\n  let bestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY\n\n  headerCells.forEach((header, index) => {\n    const distance = Math.abs(anchor - anchorFor(header, numeric))\n    if (distance < bestDistance) {\n      bestDistance = distance\n      bestIndex = index\n    }\n  })\n\n  return bestIndex\n}\n\n/**\n * Busca la fila de cabecera. Se exige que al menos una fila posterior tenga\n * una fecha parseable en la columna de fecha, para no confundir un título con\n * una cabecera.\n */\nconst findHeader = (\n  rows: readonly PdfRow[],\n  scanLimit: number\n): HeaderMatch | null => {\n  const limit = Math.min(rows.length, scanLimit)\n\n  for (let index = 0; index < limit; index += 1) {\n    const cells = rows[index].cells\n    if (cells.length < 3) continue\n\n    const mapping = mapColumns(cells.map((cell) => cell.text))\n    if (!mapping || mapping.date === null) continue\n\n    const dateColumn = mapping.date\n    const hasDateBelow = rows.slice(index + 1, index + 12).some((row) => {\n      const matrixRow = assignRow(row.cells, cells)\n      const value = matrixRow[dateColumn]\n      return Boolean(value) && parseDate(value) !== null\n    })\n\n    if (hasDateBelow) return { rowIndex: index, cells, mapping }\n  }\n\n  return null\n}\n\nconst assignRow = (\n  cells: readonly PdfCell[],\n  headerCells: readonly PdfCell[]\n): string[] => {\n  const row = new Array<string>(headerCells.length).fill(\"\")\n\n  for (const cell of cells) {\n    const column = assignColumn(headerCells, cell)\n    row[column] = row[column] ? `${row[column]} ${cell.text}` : cell.text\n  }\n\n  return row\n}\n\n/**\n * Convierte filas ya reconstruidas en transacciones.\n * Devuelve `null` si no hay cabecera reconocible o ninguna transacción.\n */\nexport const parsePdfRows = (\n  rows: readonly PdfRow[],\n  options: PdfLayoutParseOptions\n): ExtractResult | null => {\n  if (rows.length === 0) return null\n\n  const maxRows = options.maxRows ?? 5000\n  const header = findHeader(rows, options.headerScanLimit ?? 80)\n  if (!header) return null\n\n  const { cells: headerCells, mapping, rowIndex: headerIndex } = header\n  const width = headerCells.length\n  const headerTexts = headerCells.map((cell) => cell.text)\n  const headerSignature = headerTexts.map(normalizeText).join(\"|\")\n\n  const matrix: Matrix = [headerTexts]\n  const dateColumn = mapping.date\n  const descriptionColumn = mapping.description\n\n  for (let index = headerIndex + 1; index < rows.length; index += 1) {\n    if (matrix.length > maxRows) break\n\n    const row = rows[index]\n    if (row.cells.length === 0) continue\n\n    const signature = row.cells.map((cell) => normalizeText(cell.text)).join(\"|\")\n    if (signature === headerSignature) continue\n\n    const values = assignRow(row.cells, headerCells)\n    if (values.every((value) => !value)) continue\n\n    const dateValue = dateColumn === null ? 79:       bestDistance = distance
80:       bestIndex = index
81:     }
82:   })
83: 
84:   return bestIndex
85: }
86: 
87: /**
88:  * Busca la fila de cabecera. Se exige que al menos una fila posterior tenga
89:  * una fecha parseable en la columna de fecha, para no confundir un título con
90:  * una cabecera.
91:  */
92: const findHeader = (
93:   rows: readonly PdfRow[],
94:   scanLimit: number
95: ): HeaderMatch | null => {
96:   const limit = Math.min(rows.length, scanLimit)
97: 
98:   for (let index = 0; index < limit; index += 1) {
99:     const cells = rows[index].cells
100:     if (cells.length < 3) continue
101: 
102:     const mapping = mapColumns(cells.map((cell) => cell.text))
103:     if (!mapping || mapping.date === null) continue
104: 
105:     const dateColumn = mapping.date
106:     const hasDateBelow = rows.slice(index + 1, index + 12).some((row) => {
107:       const matrixRow = assignRow(row.cells, cells)
108:       const value = matrixRow[dateColumn]
109:       return Boolean(value) && parseDate(value) !== null
110:     })
111: 
112:     if (hasDateBelow) return { rowIndex: index, cells, mapping }
113:   }
114: 
115:   return null
116: }
117: 
118: const assignRow = (
119:   cells: readonly PdfCell[],
120:   headerCells: readonly PdfCell[]
121: ): string[] => {
122:   const row = new Array<string>(headerCells.length).fill("")
123: 
124:   for (const cell of cells) {
125:     const column = assignColumn(headerCells, cell)
126:     row[column] = row[column] ? `${row[column]} ${cell.text}` : cell.text
127:   }
128: 
129:   return row
130: }
131: 
132: /**
133:  * Convierte filas ya reconstruidas en transacciones.
134:  * Devuelve `null` si no hay cabecera reconocible o ninguna transacción.
135:  */
136: export const parsePdfRows = (
137:   rows: readonly PdfRow[],
138:   options: PdfLayoutParseOptions
139: ): ExtractResult | null => {
140:   if (rows.length === 0) return null
141: 
142:   const maxRows = options.maxRows ?? 5000
143:   const header = findHeader(rows, options.headerScanLimit ?? 80)
144:   if (!header) return null
145: 
146:   const { cells: headerCells, mapping, rowIndex: headerIndex } = header
147:   const width = headerCells.length
148:   const headerTexts = headerCells.map((cell) => cell.text)
149:   const headerSignature = headerTexts.map(normalizeText).join("|")
150: 
151:   const matrix: Matrix = [headerTexts]
152:   const dateColumn = mapping.date
153:   const descriptionColumn = mapping.description
154: 
155:   for (let index = headerIndex + 1; index < rows.length; index += 1) {
156:     if (matrix.length > maxRows) break
157: 
158:     const row = rows[index]
159:     if (row.cells.length === 0) continue
160: 
161:     const signature = row.cells.map((cell) => normalizeText(cell.text)).join("|")
162:     if (signature === headerSignature) continue
163: 
164:     const values = assignRow(row.cells, headerCells)
165:     if (values.every((value) => !value)) continue
166: 
167:     const dateValue = dateColumn === null ? "" : values[dateColumn] ?? ""
168: 
169:     if (!dateValue || parseDate(dateValue) === null) {
170:       // Posible continuación de una descripción partida en dos renglones.
171:       const filled = values
172:         .map((value, column) => ({ value, column }))
173:         .filter((entry) => entry.value)
174: 
175:       if (
176:         descriptionColumn !== null &&
177:         filled.length === 1 &&
178:         filled[0].column === descriptionColumn &&
179:         matrix.length > 1
180:       ) {
181:         const previous = matrix[matrix.length - 1]
182:         previous[descriptionColumn] =
183:           `${previous[descriptionColumn]} ${filled[0].value}`.trim()
184:       }
185: 
186:       // Cabeceras de página, pies, totales y resúmenes se descartan.
187:       continue
188:     }
189: 
190:     matrix.push(values)
191:   }
192: 
193:   if (matrix.length <= 1) return null
194: 
195:   const table = parseTable(matrix, {
196:     fileName: options.fileName,
197:     source: "pdf",
198:     firstRowNumber: 1,
199:   })
200: 
201:   if (table.transactions.length === 0) return null
202: 
203:   const rawText = pdfRowsToText(rows)
204: 
205:   return {
206:     transactions: table.transactions,
207:     errors: table.errors,
208:     warnings: table.warnings,
209:     source: "pdf",
210:     fileName: options.fileName,
211:     rawText,
212:     detection: {
213:       format: "pdf",
214:       bank: options.bank ?? null,
215:       currency: table.currency ?? detectCurrencyFromText(rawText),
216:       confidence: table.mapping ? 0.85 : 0,
217:       headerRow: table.headerRow,
218:       delimiter: null,
219:       encoding: null,
220:       sheetName: null,
221:       columns: mappingToColumnIndexes(table.mapping),
222:       balanceChecked: table.balanceChecked,
223:     },
224:   }
225: }
226: 
227: /**
228:  * Recibe los `TextItem` de cada página (tal como los entrega pdf.js) y
229:  * devuelve el resultado o `null` si el PDF no tiene una tabla reconocible.
230:  */
231: export const parsePdfPages = (
232:   pages: readonly (readonly unknown[])[],
233:   options: PdfLayoutParseOptions
234: ): ExtractResult | null => {
235:   const rows = pages.flatMap((items) => buildPdfRows(items))
236:   return parsePdfRows(rows, options)
237: }

{
  "path": "D:\\Proyectos de Programación\\financial-control\\lib\\parsers\\file-extractor.ts",
  "oldString": "/**\n * Extrae texto del PDF en el cliente usando pdfjs-dist.\n *\n * Fase 3 reemplazará el regex por reconstrucción posicional (X/Y), pero el\n * fallback actual se mantiene para no perder cobertura mientras tanto.\n */\nconst extractPdfTransactions = async (\n  file: File,\n  fileName: string\n): Promise<ExtractResult> => {\n  // Import dinámico para evitar problemas de SSR con pdfjs-dist\n  const pdfjsLib = await import(\"pdfjs-dist\")\n  const worker = await import(\"pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs\")\n\n  if (pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc === undefined) {\n    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = (worker as any).default || worker\n  }\n\n  const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()\n  const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer }).promise\n\n  let fullText = \"\"\n  for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {\n    const page = await pdf.getPage(i)\n    const content = await page.getTextContent()\n    const pageText = content.items\n      .map((item: any) => (item as any).str ?? \"\")\n      .join(\" \")\n    fullText += pageText + \"\\n\"\n  }",
  "newString": "/**\n * Extrae transacciones de un PDF en el cliente usando pdfjs-dist.\n *\n * Estrategia:\n *   1. Se recolectan los `TextItem` de cada página (con su X/Y) y se intenta\n *      la reconstrucción posicional (`parsePdfPages`), que reutiliza el motor\n *      genérico de tablas.\n *   2. Si el PDF no tiene una tabla reconocible, se cae al parser por regex\n *      de Atlántida sobre el texto plano.\n */\nconst extractPdfTransactions = async (\n  file: File,\n  fileName: string\n): Promise<ExtractResult> => {\n  // Import dinámico para evitar problemas de SSR con pdfjs-dist\n  const pdfjsLib = await import(\"pdfjs-dist\")\n  const worker = await import(\"pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs\")\n\n  if (pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc === undefined) {\n    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = (worker as any).default || worker\n  }\n\n  const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer()\n  const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer }).promise\n\n  let fullText = \"\"\n  const pages: unknown[][] = []\n\n  for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {\n    const page = await pdf.getPage(i)\n    const content = await page.getTextContent()\n    const items: unknown[] = [...content.items]\n    pages.push(items)\n\n    const pageText = items\n      .map((item) => (item as { str?: string }).str ?? \"\")\n      .join(\" \")\n    fullText += pageText + \"\\n\"\n  }"
}

## Assistant

_Thinking:_

So `basura,Otro,100.00` has an amount. A summary row like `Total,,1234.56` also has an amount. So distinguishing by "has amount" won't preserve L-18.

Distinguish by keyword in description/date: if the date cell text matches summary keywords (saldo anterior, saldo final, total, subtotal, total del período, gran total) or the description matches, skip silently. "basura" isn't a keyword → still error. That's safe.

But is it desirable? Requirements said "Detección 100% genérica". Silently skipping "Total" rows avoids noisy errors. Risk: hiding an actual data row whose date is a keyword? Very unlikely.

However, changing this now while the PDF subagent runs — the PDF tests don't test parseTable summary rows directly except through parsePdfRows where rows without dates never reach parseTable. So changing table.ts would be invisible to their test... unless they test case 12 via parsePdfRows. It'd still pass.

But any change to core/table.ts might alter their observed results mid-run (they run vitest multiple times). Risk: they might report flaky failure. It's a low-risk change but I'd rather wait for their completion to avoid confusion. Meanwhile I can update FEATURES.md.

40: ## 3. Flujo actual (tal como funciona hoy)
41: 
42: ```
43: UploadBulkTransactionForm.tsx (client)
44:   └─ handleFileChange → extractTransactions(file)          [lib/parsers/file-extractor.ts]
45:        ├─ getFileSource(fileName)  → solo por extensión (.csv/.xlsx/.xls/.pdf)
46:        ├─ csv   → parseCsv(file, fileName)                 [lib/parsers/csv-parser.ts]
47:        ├─ excel → parseExcel(file, fileName)               [lib/parsers/excel-parser.ts]
48:        └─ pdf   → extractPdfTransactions()
49:                     └─ pdfjs-dist getTextContent() → items.map(str).join(" ")
50:                        └─ parseAtlántidaPDF(text, fileName)  [lib/parsers/atlantida-pdf-parser.ts]
51:   └─ setTransactions(result.transactions) → tabla editable (fecha, desc, tipo, monto, ref, categoría)
52:        └─ "Suggest categories with AI" → suggestTransactionCategories() [server/transactions/ai-actions.ts]
53:        └─ submit → formDispatcher → createBulkTransactions() [server/transactions/actions.ts]
54:             └─ bulkTransactionSchema.safeParse por fila → insert por chunks → revalidatePath
55: ```
56: 
57: ### Archivos clave y su rol
58: 
59: | Archivo | Rol |
60: |---|---|
61: | `lib/parsers/types.ts` | `ParsedTransaction`, `ExtractResult`, `ParserSource` |
62: | `lib/parsers/file-extractor.ts` | Orquestador + extracción de texto del PDF con pdfjs |
63: | `lib/parsers/csv-parser.ts` | Papa 5.7, `header:true`, mapeo de columnas |
64: | `lib/parsers/excel-parser.ts` | XLSX 0.18.5, `sheet_to_json(header:1)`, detección de fila de cabecera |
65: | `lib/parsers/atlantida-pdf-parser.ts` | 2 regex globales sobre texto plano |
66: | `lib/parsers/pdf-worker.d.ts` | Shim de tipos para `pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs` |
67: | `app/(app)/transactions/_components/UploadBulkTransactionForm.tsx` | UI de carga, tabla editable, toasts, submit |
68: | `app/(app)/transactions/schema/index.ts` | `bulkTransactionSchema` (validación por fila) |
69: | `server/transactions/actions.ts` | `createBulkTransactions` (línea ~319) |
70: | `tests/unit/parsers.spec.ts` | 31 tests L-13…L-31 |
71: 
72: ### Restricciones del schema que afectan a los parsers
73: 
74: `app/(app)/transactions/schema/index.ts` → `bulkTransactionSchema`:
75: 
76: - `amount`: string, regex `^\d+(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?$` → **no acepta negativos ni `0.00` con signo; `0.00` sí pasa**.
77: - `transactionDescription`: `.max(255)` → descripciones de PDF pueden reventar fila por fila.
78: - `transactionDate`: `YYYY-MM-DD` + refine con `new Date(...)` → **`2026-02-31` se normaliza a 3 de marzo y pasa** (por eso la validación de calendario debe hacerse en el parser).
79: - `categoryId` es NOT NULL en DB: si falta se usa la categoría por defecto.
80: - `userId` y `accountId` se resuelven en el servidor (no se confía en el cliente).
81:

94: ## 5. Hallazgos / bugs confirmados (base del trabajo)
95: 
96: ### Dinero (`cleanAmount`, duplicado en 3 archivos)
97: | Entrada | Hoy | Esperado |
98: |---|---|---|
99: | `1.234` | `1.23` | `1234.00` |
100: | `(1,234.56)` | `1234.56` (+) | negativo |
101: | `500.00-` | `500.00` (+) | negativo |
102: | `−500.00` (U+2212) | positivo/0 | negativo |
103: | `1.234.567` | `0.00` | `1234567.00` |
104: | basura | `"0"` | `null` (distinguir de cero) |
105: 
106: - Moneda solo soporta `L $ €`; el resto se pierde por descarte de caracteres.
107: - El tipo se calcula con una **segunda** expresión distinta de `cleanAmount` → signos incoherentes.
108: - Débito/crédito: si `debit` viene negativo no entra en ninguna rama → transacción `amount: "0.00"` **sin error**.
109: 
110: ### Fechas (`normalizeDate`)
111: - Sin validación de calendario: `31/02/2026` → `2026-02-31`, y el schema lo acepta.
112: - Solo 3 formatos: `YYYY-MM-DD`, `DD/MM/YYYY`, `YYYY-Mmm-DD`.
113: - Faltan: `DD-MM-YYYY`, `DD.MM.YYYY`, `YYYY/MM/DD`, `DD/MM/YY`, ISO con hora, `7 Sep 2026`, `Sep 7, 2026`, `07-SEP-26`, meses en inglés.
114: - Serial de Excel solo en `excel-parser` y limitado a `40000..60000`; CSV no lo soporta.
115: 
116: ### CSV / Excel
117: - CSV asume cabecera en línea 1 → falla con preámbulo, `sep=;`, BOM.
118: - `file.text()` fuerza UTF-8 → CSV CP1252/Latin-1 dan `DescripciÃ³n` y pierde la columna.
119: - `findColumnIndex` por `includes` y orden de candidatos → "Monto original"/"Total" pueden ganar; no distingue moneda extranjera.
120: - Sinónimos faltantes: `debe/haber`, `cargos/abonos`, `retiros/depósitos`, `egresos/ingresos`, `importe`, `withdrawal/deposit`, `paid in/out`.
121: - Excel: solo primera hoja; cabecera debe ser exactamente `fecha`/`date`/`transactiondate`; `rowNum = i + 2` desfasado (+5 con preámbulo de Atlántida).
122: - `result.errors` de Papa se descarta; el callback `error` es casi inalcanzable con string.
123: - `if (!description && !bankReference)` es código muerto (`|| "No description"`).
124: - No se usa la columna `Saldo` → sin validación cruzada.
125: 
126: ### PDF
127: - `items.map(str).join(" ")` destruye líneas/columnas antes de aplicar regex.
128: - `FORMATO_2_REGEX` sin ancla de fin de línea → descripción con número final puede desalinear débito/crédito.
129: - `FORMATO_1_REGEX` exige literal `L `; falla con `L.`, `HNL`, `US$`.
130: - Sin continuidad entre páginas, sin filtrar pies repetidos, sin cuadre de saldo, sin PDFs cifrados/escaneados.
131: 
132: ### Transversal
133: - Sin metadatos de detección (`parser used: pdf` es todo lo que ve el usuario).
134: - Sin fallback: si no es Atlántida, falla.
135: - Sin dedupe al re-subir (tempId usa `Date.now()`/`random`).
136: - `xlsx` importado estáticamente (bundle pesado).
137:

139: 
140: ## 6. Arquitectura objetivo
141: 
142: ```
143: lib/parsers/
144:   core/
145:     money.ts      parseAmount(raw) -> { value: number|null, negative, currency }
146:     dates.ts      parseDate(raw, { dayFirst }) -> { iso, confidence } + resolveDayFirst()
147:     text.ts       decodeFileBytes() UTF-8/CP1252/UTF-16 + normalizeText() (NFKC, NBSP, sep=)
148:     table.ts      Matrix=string[][]; detectHeader, mapColumns, extractRows, reconcileBalances
149:     detection.ts  DetectionInfo, ParseWarning, scoring de formato/banco
150:   pdf/
151:     layout.ts     TextItem[] -> filas(Y) y celdas(X)
152:   adapters/
153:     atlantida.ts      solo hints (firma, orden de fecha, moneda, líneas resumen)
154:     generic-table.ts  CSV/XLSX sin cabecera conocida
155:     generic-pdf.ts    cabecera posicional + fallback regex anclado
156:   file-extractor.ts orquestador: sniff por bytes -> adapters por score -> fallback chain
157: ```
158: 
159: Principios:
160: 1. **Toda fuente se normaliza a `Matrix`** → CSV, XLSX y PDF comparten mapeo/validación.
161: 2. **Mapeo auto-validado**: se prueban hipótesis (fecha A/B, monto vs débito/crédito, swap) y gana la de mayor tasa de parseo + cuadre de saldo.
162: 3. **Cuadre de saldo** `anterior + crédito − débito ≈ saldo` como validador de columnas.
163: 4. **PDF posicional** con `transform[4]` (X), `transform[5]` (Y), `height` y `hasEOL`; regex solo como respaldo.
164: 5. `ExtractResult.detection` expone formato, banco, moneda, confianza, mapping y warnings a la UI.

229: ## 8. APIs verificadas en las versiones instaladas
230: 
231: `package.json`: `papaparse ^5.7.0`, `xlsx ^0.18.5`, `pdfjs-dist ^5.6.205`, `next 16.1.7`, `react 19.2.4`, `zod ^4.4.3`, `vitest ^4.1.11`.
232: 
233: - **pdfjs-dist 5.6.205**: `TextItem` incluye `str`, `dir`, `transform: any[]`, `width`, `height`, `fontName`, `hasEOL` (`node_modules/pdfjs-dist/types/src/display/api.d.ts` líneas ~338-357). `getTextContent()` permite reconstrucción posicional.
234: - `GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = (await import("pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs")).default` (ya usado en `file-extractor.ts`).
235: - **Papa 5.7**: `header`, `transformHeader`, `skipEmptyLines`, `delimiter`, `delimitersToGuess`, `beforeFirstChunk`; `result.errors` disponible y hoy ignorado.
236: - **XLSX 0.18.5**: `XLSX.read(buffer, { type: "array" })`, `utils.sheet_to_json(ws, { header: 1, defval: "", raw: true })`.
